    Recursion: root seeds, structural moves, navigators & drivers #

    Root seeds & co-construction cycles (Reflections 258, 259, 302, 307, 318, 346–349, 504, 506, 508) #

    • UniversalProducerRootSeed — Reflections 258 + 259 (a universal producer's first brick is its ROOT SEED — the positional packaging of its one already-available instance, built as a faithful mirror of the flat-deliverable characterization with the payload swapped: once the consumer is folded (R257), the producer starts at the recursion's base case, not the recursion. charRec is the verbatim mirror of charFlat — same subst/drop_pre body, the flat payload Flat swapped for the recursive Rec; root_instance shows the seed IS the lo = pre.length (outer-window) instance of the universal UnivRec, the rest deferred to the navigation recursion (so UnivRec is left open). Positive rec_good/seed_root on good = [OB,A,CB] with a length-1 prefix recovered by drop 1; negative not_rec_empty on empty = [] via the Rec ⟹ Flat projection — the recursive deliverable can never produce an empty block, so the seed never fires there. R259 map extension: the seed mirrors verbatim across the seq→map collection axis — charMap is the verbatim copy of charRec (payload Rec swapped for the parallel RecMap), the positional packaging step being collection-agnostic; the only deltas live in the feed, the R246 storage asymmetry one tier up at the seed's hypothesis count — MapFeed carries an extra ska precondition (toy of simpleKeyAllowed) and an extra floor : 3 ≤ length (toy of the R250 3 ≤ n map floor), BOTH absent on SeqFeed, and seedMap ignores them exactly as the real lemma ignores _h_n3. The floor is load-bearing: seqfeed_singleton : SeqFeed [A] is inhabited but no_mapfeed_singleton : ¬ MapFeed [A] (3 ≤ 1 false), the seq side having no such floor; positive seed_map_root on good, negative not_recmap_empty via the RecMap ⟹ Flat projection)
    • RootSeedRecursiveProducerSwap — Reflection 302 (the ROOT SEED of a recursive-deliverable producer reuses the FLAT derivation's slice/decomposition verbatim and swaps only the body producer; the slice bridge is PRODUCER-AGNOSTIC — the toy of seqRoot_safeBodyUnit. A wrap body := 0 :: (body ++ [99]) container framing and its windowSlice l := (l.take (l.length-1)).drop 1; KEY windowSlice_wrap : windowSlice (wrap body) = body is about the CONTAINER SHAPE, never any property of body — so the ROOT SEED rootSeed (P) (h : P body) : P (windowSlice (wrap body)) is generic over the producer P (mirror of h_take_eq transporting any body fact). Flat = all-positive (toy WellTyped), Rec = the structural all-positive chain (toy RecSeqBody), Rec.toFlat the projection. flatRoot/recRoot are the two instances — swap only P; recRoot_reprojects (nothing lost — Rec.toFlat re-projects the flat fact). KEY agnosticism witnesses slice_agnostic_offFlat : windowSlice (wrap [0,5]) = [0,5] + flat_fails_offFlat : ¬ Flat [0,5] (the slice holds for a body where Flat FAILS — it is about the framing, not the content). The lesson: to land the root seed of a recursive-deliverable producer, find the existing flat-fact derivation, observe its slice identity is producer-agnostic, re-run it swapping the recursive body producer for the flat one, project through the unchanged slice — one chain-replay you already have a template for, not a new induction)
    • RootSeedDiscriminatorNotFromGate — Reflection 307 (the ROOT seed of a universal-over-gated-windows producer carries a DISCRIMINATOR the gate cannot supply — the toy of seqEnclosingFacts_provider_root, the (i'-b-locator) base case. A universal producer ∀ window, Gate window → Deliverable window is split into a ROOT seed + a DESCENT (per [[ref-universal-producer-root-seed-first]]); at the root the deliverable is infra-supplied. The non-obvious part: the root seed does NOT consume the universal's Gate — the gate is satisfied by seq-typed windows at ANY depth, including the descent's own targets, so it cannot tell a root-level window from a nested one. The root seed instead carries the top-level discriminator AtRoot (toy flowBracketBalance tokens 2 a = 0), strictly STRONGER than Gate and NOT derivable from it — correct, because it is the SAME quantity the descent re-establishes per level. Toy Win carries bal (balance from the outer origin; 0 ⇒ root level) + seqTyped (the gate); Deliverable w := ∃ encl, encl.seqTyped ∧ w.bal = encl.bal (an enclosing seq window at whose level w sits — the re-seated discriminator). descentStep takes the LOCATED enclosing as a hypothesis (in reality recseqentry_seqbracket_oracle); rootSeed := descentStep … outerWin … hr makes the shared discriminator literal — the root seed IS the descent step specialised to the outer window, AtRoot plugged into the re-seat slot. POSITIVE: rootSeed rootWin atRoot_root on ⟨0,true⟩; #guard rootWin.bal == 0. NEGATIVE (the gate does not supply the discriminator): gate_nested : Gate ⟨2,true⟩ holds yet not_atRoot_nested : ¬ AtRoot ⟨2,true⟩, and outer_wrong_for_nested : ¬ (nestedWin.bal = outerWin.bal) — the outer window is the WRONG enclosing for a nested window, so the root seed is inapplicable and the nested case needs the descent's located enclosing (descentStep nestedWin ⟨2,true⟩ rfl rfl). Contrast [[ref-guarded-universal-fold-relocates-guard]] (there the guard is the producer's GIFT narrowing its domain; here the discriminator is the seed's DEBT, a hypothesis it cannot discharge locally))
    • NarrowFromRootBreaksRederivationCycle — Reflection 318 (re-deriving a SUBSET-RESTRICTION carrier per recursion-window is CIRCULAR if its per-window input comes from the recursion's own output; seed it ONCE at the root from the FLAT producer and narrow down — the toy of seqRoot_seqInteriorSeparators and the de-risk that corrected R317. The seq windowWidth_strongRecOn step needs SeqInteriorSeparators tokens lo hi per window (it supplies the separator facts flowBodyContent_of_deep cannot project). R317 planned to RE-DERIVE the carrier per window via a dispatcher needing that window's own SafeBodyUnit — but at a descended window the only SafeBodyUnit source is RecSeqBody.toSafeBodyUnit of that window's own RecSeqBody, the VERY OUTPUT the step produces: circular. The carrier is a subset restriction (SeqInteriorSeparators_narrow), so seed it once at the root from the FLAT seqRoot_safeBodyUnit (no recursion ⇒ no cycle) and narrow to every sub-window; the per-window helper (RecSeqBody.toSafeBodyUnit, the circular edge) is DROPPED. PROVEN nugget: C lo hi := ∀ a b, lo ≤ a → a ≤ b → b ≤ hi → Q a b; C_narrow (the subset-restriction edge, two Nat.le_trans); C_all_of_root (root [0,N] ⇒ every [lo,hi] with hi ≤ N); flat_root (the root seed, established directly with no recursion). POSITIVE (example : C 2 4): the root seed at [0,5] covers the nested sub-window [2,4) for free (the de-risk coverage claim — the window lies WITHIN the root span). NEGATIVE (#guard): model the step P w := step (C w) reading the carrier; the FLAT path pFromFlat w = step (flatC w) is window-AWARE (pFromFlat 3 = 4, pFromFlat 7 = 8), while the CIRCULAR re-derivation pCirc (C w := extract (P w), P w := step (C w), fuel-exposed) is window-BLIND — pCirc 100 3 = pCirc 100 7 = 1, the same regardless of w and never the genuine value (!(pCirc 100 3 == pFromFlat 3)): the cycle injects no real data, only the flat root seed carries the per-window datum.     • CarrierProvenanceNotType — Reflection 346 (a "narrow the root carrier" cycle-break works only if the carrier has an emission-INDEPENDENT producer; narrowability is provenance-blind — the PROBE behind (i'-b-B2c-carrier-narrow), R345's resolution route.     • ParentRelativeGConjunct — Reflection 347 (a PARENT-RELATIVE recursion-guard conjunct rides the recursion's structural SITES, not the uniform IH adapter — the PROBE that settled the live route for (i'-b-B2c-ih-recseqbody-step), R346's handoff. R346 left two candidate carrier-free routes for the step's per-window FlowBodyContent: (A) read its separator facts off the window's OWN recursive output (seqSeparatorFacts_of_recseqbody), or (B) thread FlowBodyContent as a fifth G-conjunct propagated by the LANDED edges seqRoot_flowBodyContent (root, emission-seeded) / flowBodyContent_descend (646) / flowBodyContent_advance (4205). PROBE picks (B). FINDING: FlowBodyContent's descend edge CONSUMES THE PARENT–CHILD RELATION (it takes the parent's h_content + the opener/close/floor), so the conjunct CANNOT be re-established in the dispatch's UNIFORM IH adapter (recseqentry_window_dispatch:5618/5629, which sees only the child's INTRINSIC four facts FlowBodyWindow ∧ FlowBodyContentDeep ∧ SeqEnclosed ∧ close, never how the child descends) — this is the R343 [[ref-context-free-adapter-knot]] knot, now RESOLVED: the four existing conjuncts are themselves supplied not in the adapter but at the recursion's STRUCTURAL CALL SITES (the seq oracle's descend, the assembler's advance), where the parent–child relation IS in scope; the adapter merely FORWARDS. So WIDEN the dispatch's IH SIGNATURE by the F conjunct and discharge it at those two sites via the landed edges. Route (A) is off the critical path: reading F off the window's OWN output is a same-window cycle for the producing window (output ≠ input) and redundant for children (the descend edge already builds it). The conjunct is NECESSARY: a nested window needs its own bodySucc, sourced only from the parent's F via descend — in scope only if F is threaded parent→child. Toy: F inductive (root no-parent | descend needs-parent); Intrinsic (the adapter's only data). POSITIVE (F_root_seed + F_supply_at_descend): the root seed and the structural-site supply both build F where the parent (or none) is in scope. NEGATIVE (F_nonroot_carries_parent): a nested F CARRIES a strictly-smaller parent (inversion), so no Intrinsic lo hi → F lo hi exists — the uniform adapter cannot manufacture it. NEGATIVE (own_output_is_a_loop): route (A) at the current window is an endo F → F with no seed, echoing R346's carrier_route_is_a_loop. RESOLVES the knot [[ref-context-free-adapter-knot]] diagnosed; corrects R346's framing — seqSeparatorFacts_of_recseqbody is off-path, the threaded FlowBodyContent edges are the route; applies [[ref-universal-producer-root-seed-first]] + [[ref-push-blind-frame-dependent]] (parent-dependent vs parent-blind edges))
    • ProjectionUpStrengthOrder — Reflection 348 (a carrier-free IH thread's DESCEND edge re-enters its OWN window: the five→four IH projection runs UP the strength order, and the combined deliverable RELOCATES the same-window cycle without breaking it — the PROBE that settled (i'-b-B2c-dispatch-ih-widen) step (3), run mechanically sorry-free in Tests/Guards/Proofs/DescendIHProjectionProbe.lean against the real defs. FINDING 1 (STRICT STRENGTH ORDER): IH4 (child Out/RecSeqBody from four INTRINSIC facts) is STRICTLY STRONGER than IH5 (needs the child's F too) — four→five is a free drop (ih5_of_ih4), five→four closes IFF a per-window provider : Intrinsic → F exists (ih4_of_ih5_iff_provider), so the needed projection runs UP the strength order, impossible without a NEW producer for the dropped F — the carrier-free F the arc lacks (R296/R345). FINDING 2 (CYCLE SURVIVES COMBINATION): F n is read OFF Out n (f_of_out, toy seqSeparatorFacts_of_recseqbody) while Out n's dispatch needs F n (out_needs_f); composed that is an endo with no seed (same_window_loop, R346's carrier_route_is_a_loop at ONE window), and the combined Pair n := Out n ∧ F n does not dissolve it (combined_step_still_cyclic — both fields trace through the same F n input) — combination relocates the cycle from the recursive edge INTO the step. THE BREAK (break_via_emission): only an EMISSION source of F independent of Out opens it — generalize the flat root seed (seqRoot_safeBodyUnit/seqRoot_flowBodyContent) to nested sub-values, making F a genuine four-conjunct-G INPUT. EDGE ASYMMETRY (the locus): advance_supplies_child is a clean parent→child arrow (NO Out in the loop, R347's optimistic shape — flowBodyContent_advance re-bases, no IH); descend_supplies_child_via_out routes the child's F through the child's own Out (seqChild_safeBodyUnit), which out_needs_f ties back — a same-window 2-cycle, because bodySucc has no all-depth balance-free form (R296) so descend cannot re-base down a level. Corrects R347's "landed edges" optimism (its toy abstracted descend as clean parent→child, hiding the Out re-entry); applies [[ref-push-blind-frame-dependent]] / [[ref-converse-forward-invariant-asymmetry]]; same-window loop is [[ref-carrier-provenance-not-type]]'s shape)
    • DescopeIsRouteRelative — Reflection 349 (a producer DESCOPED as "an alternative, not a necessity" UN-DESCOPES once the route that subsumed it is the one being eliminated; the emission seed EXTRACTS position-blind, it does not re-scan — the PROBE that settled (i'-b-B2c-nested-fbc-emission-seed), with the offset gap #guard-confirmed in Tests/Guards/Proofs/SeqNestedEmissionSeedProbe.lean. FINDING 1 (POSITION-BLIND EXTRACTION): every RecSeqBody/RecSeqEntry/SafeBodyUnit/ContentStartTok constraint is .val-only, so the nested interior RecSeqBody STORED in the flat root seqRoot_recseqbody's RecSeqEntry.seq.h_rec field sits over the EXACT outer-array slice (right .poss) — the ASSEMBLE is LANDABLE NOW from an emission RecSeqEntry via recseqentry_seq_extract + interior_window_eq + .toSafeBodyUnit (nestedSeq_safeBodyUnit_of_entry, sorry-free, NO carrier/recursion).     • ContentSurfacingLocatorTwin — Reflection 504 (surface a LOCATOR's buried witness to propagate a free child projection one level up. R503 ([[BundleFreeProjectionIntoProducer]]) bundled the free child FlowBodyContent into the seq oracle; the oracle's emitted child content lives at the descend close j, but the located form recseqentry_seqbracket_located runs the close-locator matchingClose_full_seq and reports only the ENTRY-shaped ∃ m, RecSeqEntry …, whose boundary m is the entry END (j or j+1), so j is BURIED. HONEST SCOPE — the brick does NOT advance the carrier-free recursion's descend. Reading seqWindowRecSeqBody_seq_general against seqChild_safeBodyUnit_seq settles the architecture: that recursion's guard G is ALREADY content-free (FlowBodyWindow ∧ FlowBodyContentDeepSeq ∧ SeqEnclosed ∧ close ∧ bounds), and the carrier SeqInteriorSeparators is consumed in EXACTLY ONE place — sourcing the CURRENT window's FlowBodyContent. The descend itself is content-FREE: the seq oracle draws the child RecSeqBody from the content-free width IH (seqChild_safeBodyUnit_seq calls the oracle ONCE on (lo+1, j)), so the descended child needs NO content. Hence R503's and this brick's surfaced child content is genuinely free but NOT consumed by the descend recursive call. The ONE real obstacle is isolated: the CURRENT window's content, intrinsically TOP-DOWN / path-dependent — R500 flowBodyContent_descend_seq produces a child's content from the PARENT's content — so a pure windowWidth_strongRecOn (bottom-up by WIDTH, no parent) cannot thread it. Closing it carrier-free needs P := FlowBodyContent → RecSeqBody (content as ANTECEDENT) with a nested content-free body IH carrying parent content, or a custom recursor on the descent carrying parent content — a JOINT/mutual construction, not single-theorem wiring.     • LocatedContainerRead — Reflection 506 (a navigator LOCATES a container that STORES the deliverable; reading the deliverable — and its downstream projection — out of the located container is FREE, so the navigator's job collapses to LOCATION, not PRODUCTION.     • CrossFrameStructuralDescent — Reflection 508 (the path-FREE route the R507 residual ([[ProducerGateCoversFragment]]) needs: the root RecSeqBody (off emission, seqRoot_recseqbody) ALREADY STORES every nested window's body as a subterm of the mutual inductive — RecSeqEntry.seq stores RecSeqBody interior, RecSeqEntry.mapRec stores RecMapBody interior, a RecMapPair stores its key/value blocks as RecSeqEntrys — so descending the INDUCTIVE reaches every window regardless of the bracket PATH; the SeqPathAllSeq gate is an artifact of TOKEN-SPINE walking, not of the deliverable.     Structural moves & shape dispatch (Reflections 234, 235, 260–271, 364, 509, 523, 525–527, 606) #

    • RecursiveDeliverableProjectToFlat — Reflections 234 + 235 (recursive deliverable, project-to-flat-first; single-level descent step)
    • DescendStepConsumerJoint — Reflections 260 + 261 (the navigation recursion's descend step IS a consumer joint's internal computation, truncated: returned as a value instead of consumed — and its symmetric map mirror. A consumer joint that peels/decomposes a located entry, descends one nesting level, then consumes the descended sub-deliverable into a terminal already contains the producer-side descend primitive its dual recursion needs — the recursion needs that sub-deliverable as a value for its IH, not the consumed terminal, so the descend step is the same proof body truncated before the consume step. Toy RecEntry/RecBody (mutual): seq_interior is the shared single-level descent; recbody_of_entry is the descend primitive returning RecBody interior ∨ interior = []; flat_of_entry is the consumer joint — defined literally as (recbody_of_entry …).imp RecBody.toFlat id, so the factoring is manifest: the joint = the descend primitive followed by the toFlat consume step, the primitive = the joint with that step truncated. Positive descend_one returns the recursable RecBody [A] while consume_one keeps only the projected Flat [A]; descend_twice descends the doubly-nested [OB,OB,A,CB,CB] two levels using only the primitive (the joint's Flat cannot be re-descended); negative no_descend_scalar — a scalar entry [A] has no OB :: (… ++ [CB]) bracket shape, so the descent never fires. R261 map mirror (## The map mirror section): MapEntry/MapBody is the seq toy with NO scalar constructor — every entry is a bracket frame — so map_interior is one case shorter than seq_interior (no scalar to exclude), the toy reflection of the real map descend mirror's dropped h_op opener-guard hypothesis; mapbody_of_entry/flat_of_map_entry/descend_map_one/descend_map_twice are verbatim mirrors of the seq witnesses, and negative no_mapentry_scalar — a bare scalar [A] is not a MapEntry at all (the structural fact behind the dropped guard))
    • StructuralMovesAdvance — Reflection 262, 263, 269 & 270 (the locate recursion's structural moves — descend + build + advance + terminate — one positional window lift per CONSTRUCTOR of the deliverable, base case included. A recursion producing a recursive-deliverable inductive needs exactly one positional move per constructor of the deliverable — not merely per way the constructors compose; for a body-of-entries deliverable (single/cons) that is DESCEND (into a nested entry's interior, eliminator side), BUILD (the same composition, constructor side — descend's dual), ADVANCE (chain entries via RBody.cons), and TERMINATE (the single base constructor). Once every constructor's lift lands — each a cheap verified brick — the recursion's residual collapses to the purely analytical entry-boundary location (the split points the moves consume). Subsidiary: the RBody.cons side-fields h_ne/h_head are free structural projections of the entry (REntry.ne_nil/REntry.head_cstart), not threaded obligations. Toy REntry/RBody (mutual) with a real cons body constructor: recbody_of_entry is DESCEND, entry_of_recbody is BUILD, recbody_cons is ADVANCE (discharging the two projections). The positives compose all moves on a multi-entry nested body — body_AFA builds [A,FE,A] by ADVANCE, entry_AFA wraps it by BUILD into [OB,A,FE,A,CB], descend_multi DESCENDs back to the multi-entry interior, body_full ADVANCEs again; negatives no_entry_empty/no_entry_separator — an entry is never empty and never a bare separator, so the moves never misfire. Extended by Reflection 263 with the map mirror of the advance step: a toy MBody/MPair (a body chains pairs KY :: (block_k ++ VL :: block_v) along FE), mbody_cons is the verbatim seq→map mirror of recbody_cons — only the terminal constructor (MBody.cons over an MPair) and the head side-field differ; the R246/R263 storage asymmetry surfaces as the simpler head projection MPair.head_key (one cases, single equality = KY, vs REntry.head_cstart's four-case CStart disjunction); positives pairAA/mbodyAA/mbody_two (ADVANCE builds a two-pair body), negatives no_mpair_empty/no_mpair_no_key — a pair is never empty and its head is pinned to KY. Extended by Reflection 269 with the TERMINATE move the original trio missed: RBody has two constructors (single/cons) but only cons had a lift, so the recursion had a step and no base; recbody_single lifts the single base constructor — the move ADVANCE defers to (its h_rest is dischargeable only at the last item). It is the cheapest move: RBody.single is indexed by the entry list verbatim, so it needs no positional plumbing (vs recbody_cons's e ++ FE :: rest) and is axiom-clean [propext] — the complexity law that a move's cost = the gap between its constructor's index and the raw window, here nil. body_AFA/body_AFAFA now show the genuine recursion shape ADVANCE ∘ … ∘ TERMINATE; mbody_single is the verbatim map mirror, the R263 storage asymmetry recurring at the base move's head projection. Extended by Reflection 270 (the map TERMINATE move's real-code landing, recmapbody_single_window): a base-case mirror is free — a mirror's cost is the plumbing it transports, and a base case has none, so the map terminate differs from the seq one only in the leaf constructor + projection family (axiom profile read off the original: both [propext]). mbody_PFPFP is the map-side recursion-shape witness — ADVANCE ∘ ADVANCE ∘ TERMINATE over a three-pair body, the structural twin of the seq body_AFAFA, identical move composition with MBody/MPair/mbody_* swapped for RBody/REntry/recbody_*)
    • EntryBoundaryLocator — Reflections 264, 265, 266, 267, 268 & 271 (the analytical entry-boundary location splits into an input side — locate the split point — and a shape side — classify the item; the input side is a single axis-agnostic combinatorial brick, because the body-separator token is shared across seq and map. A general constructive least-witness locator exists_least_in_range (for a decidable P holding at start + gap, the least witness with its minimality certificate; structural induction on the gap, decidability-driven upward scan — no Nat.find, no classical choice, no well-founded recursion — verbatim the real lemma) and its specialization firstEntryBoundary: for a balanced window (bal l l.length = 0) the least depth-0 boundary marker m (balance returns to 0, and m is the window end or a separator) with the certificate that no earlier interior position is such a marker. This pins where the first item ends, separate from the shape side (the matching-close analysis that consumes m). The discriminator: a navigation brick mirrors across seq/map exactly when it names a collection-specific deliverable type; one phrased over the shared token stream (balance, the separator FE) is written once — the input side does NOT mirror, the shape side will. Input-side positives firstMarker_l1 (the locator fires on the balanced [a],b, the first marker being the separator at m = 3, witnessed concretely by #guard bal l1 3 == 0/isFE (tokAt l1 3), minimality by bal l1 1 == 1/bal l1 2 == 1) and firstMarker_l2 (the last-item branch — the window end m = 3 = length on the single item [a], no separator); least_ge3 exercises the helper on a pure-Nat predicate; input-side negatives not_marker_inside (an interior depth-≥1 position is not a marker) and cl_is_not_separator (the boundary token is FE, not the close bracket). Reflection 265 (shape side): the shape side is the family of per-constructor window-lifts on the entry inductive Entry (scalar leaf / seq recursive), and the recursive constructor's lift is the BUILD structural move (already landed), so the new work is the non-recursive leaf — entry_scalar_window (toy of recseqentry_scalar_window): the one-token window at a scalar head is an Entry.scalar (window-singleton identity via List.getElem_cons_drop/List.getElem_take/List.drop_eq_nil_of_le, head value off tokAt via List.getElem_eq_getD). Shape-side positives scalar_window_l1_1/scalar_window_l1_4 (the leaf fires at each scalar head of l1, witnessed by #guard (l1.take 2).drop 1 == [sc]); shape-side negatives op_head_is_not_scalar_leaf (an op head is the recursive constructor's job, not the leaf's) and #guard (l1.take 3).length == 3 (the bracketed first item is not the one-token leaf shape). This is where the seq/map mirror re-splits — the lemma names the entry inductive, so the map shape side's leaf is a heavier key/value pair. Reflection 266 (the leaf family): the non-recursive leaves come as a family — entry_seqempty_window (toy of recseqentry_seqempty_window) is the scalar leaf scaled by one token: the empty bracket [ ] at an op-then-cl head is an Entry.seqEmpty over the two-token window (l.take (lo+2)).drop lo (m = lo + 2, still non-recursive), proven by the same List.getElem_cons_drop peel run twice ([lo] then [lo+1]) with the same trailing-drop-nil and getElem_take/getElem_eq_getD — only the arity moved; once the first leaf is proven the rest are the same proof at a different arity. Positive seqempty_window_l3_0 (the leaf fires at the head of l3 = [op, cl, fe, sc], witnessed by #guard (l3.take 2).drop 0 == [op, cl]); negatives #guard (l3.take 2).drop 0 != [op] (two-token shape ≠ the scalar leaf's one-token shape — distinct family members) and l1_head_is_not_empty_bracket (the empty leaf needs cl immediately after op; in l1 the token after the opener is sc, the recursive seq constructor's job). Reflection 267 (the family's last member — the near-leaf): entry_map_window (toy of recseqentry_map_window) is the nested-mapping near-leaf — the recursive seq BUILD move minus one field. A distinct map-bracket pair mo/mc (toy of .flowMappingStart/.flowMappingEnd) and a flat well-bracketed predicate WB l := bal l l.length = 0 model the storage decision: the Entry.map constructor STORES only WB interior (not a recursive body, R244), so it is a near-leaf (variable-width interior) that still terminates the dispatch. Its proof transports the recursive seq build's window plumbing verbatim — rest-decomposition (take (hi+1)).drop (lo+1) = (take hi).drop (lo+1) ++ [l[hi]] (List.take_add_one/List.drop_append_of_le_length), opener peel (List.getElem_cons_drop/List.getElem_take) into op :: (interior ++ [cl]) shape, head values off tokAt — differing only in the terminal constructor (Entry.map) and the one bare WB field (the one-field arity delta is the store-vs-project decision). Positive map_window_l4_0 (the near-leaf fires at the head of l4 = [mo, sc, mc], witnessed by #guard (l4.take 3).drop 0 == [mo, sc, mc] and interior_l4_is_wb : WB ((l4.take 2).drop 1)); negatives #guard (l4.take 3).drop 0 != [mo] (≥2-token shape, not the scalar leaf's one-token), unbalanced_interior_not_wb (the stored WB fails for the unbalanced [mo] — the flat fact stored in place of a body), and cl_is_not_map_close (the near-leaf needs the map closer mc, distinct from the sequence cl). With scalar + seqEmpty + map + the BUILD-move seq, the seq shape side's four-way head dispatch is complete. Reflection 268 (the map shape side — one assembler, not a dispatch): the shape side re-splits across seq/map (it names the collection-specific Pair inductive), but the map side is not a per-constructor dispatch — a map item is always a whole key/value pair ky <block_k> vl <block_v> (Pair, sole constructor Pair.mk), so there is nothing to classify. pair_window (toy of recmappair_window) is ONE assembler that (i) consumes the complete seq dispatch as its two sub-blocks — the key/value blocks are arbitrary Entrys, taken as hypotheses, so the map side adds zero per-constructor classification — and (ii) supplies the one new piece, the pair glue, as a composition of two already-landed plumbing patterns: the segment split at the vl separator (the ADVANCE plumbing List.take_append_drop/take_take/drop_drop, splitting [lo+1, m) at kv) and the opener peel of l[lo] (the BUILD/leaf peel List.getElem_cons_drop/List.getElem_take), terminated by Pair.mk. New tokens ky/vl (toy of .key/.value, delta 0). Positive pair_window_l5 (the assembler glues two scalar Entry blocks — both produced by the seq scalar leaf — into a Pair of l5 = [ky, sc, vl, sc], witnessed by #guard (l5.take 4).drop 0 == [ky, sc, vl, sc] and the sub-blocks #guard (l5.take 2).drop 1 == [sc]/#guard (l5.take 4).drop 3 == [sc]); negatives #guard (l5.take 4).drop 0 != [sc]/!= [ky] (the pair is a heavier four-token composite, not a single Entry), seq_head_is_not_key (an op sequence head is not a ky map head), and fe_is_not_value (the seq separator fe is distinct from the pair's vl marker). So the seq/map re-split surfaces as composition — ADVANCE-split ∘ BUILD-peel — not a fresh family. Reflection 271 (the ADVANCE-step invariant-preservation certificate — structural-complete ≠ runnable): the moves + locator + shape side make the recursion structurally complete, but it is not runnable until one more fact is proved — after ADVANCEing past a depth-0 separator at m, the tail [m+1, hi) is itself a valid recursive sub-instance. The structural moves are all assembly lemmas (given the pieces, build the constructor); none certifies the recursive call's precondition. advanceTail_invariant (toy of the real advanceTail_invariant) is that certificate — and like firstEntryBoundary it is axis-agnostic (names no deliverable type), so it sharpens R264's input/shape split: the input side is locate the point and certify the tail, both written once. It needs a range balance balR l lo hi (toy of flowBracketBalance), its additivity balR_compose (the slice split take (hi-lo) (drop lo) = take (mid-lo) (drop lo) ++ take (hi-mid) (drop mid) via List.take_add/List.drop_drop fed to sumD_append/foldl_add_shift) and the single-token value balR_single; then, given a balanced window with a depth-0 fe at m, it delivers (a) prefix-through-separator balanced, (b) tail balanced, (c) the tail re-bases (balR lo p = balR (m+1) p for m+1 ≤ p ≤ hi) — all by balR_compose split at m/m+1 plus the separator's zero delta, no induction. Positive advanceTail_l6 (the invariant fires on l6 = [op, sc, cl, fe, op, sc, cl], the separator at depth 0 position 3 splitting [a],[b]; re-basing exercised mid-bracket by #guard balR l6 0 5 == balR l6 4 5 — both 1 inside the tail's [b], the outer origin 0 and new origin 4 agreeing precisely because the prefix is balanced — and #guard balR l6 0 4 == 0/balR l6 4 7 == 0 for the two balance facts); negatives interior_sep_not_depth0 (an fe inside a bracket, l7 = [op, fe, cl], has balR l7 0 1 = 1 ≠ 0, so the depth-0 hypothesis correctly excludes it — a separator token at depth ≥ 1 is not a body boundary) and #guard balR l6 0 2 != balR l6 4 2 (re-basing is not a free identity — before the new origin the two frames disagree, it holds on the tail only because the prefix is balanced))
    • DemotedDispatchMetricFromStructure — Reflection 364 (demoting a recursion's DISPATCH from METRIC-keyed to STRUCTURE-keyed does not delete a downstream arm's metric fact; the fact migrates from "dispatch byproduct" to "structural derivation", and that derivation already exists as an inline block inside the metric-keyed SIBLING — lift it to a standalone brick. The emission-spine-walk locator dispatches by pure LENGTH ARITHMETIC (move_trichotomy, R350), "balance demoted to the correctness side, never the decision." But its ADVANCE arm's WellTyped supplier (R363) consumes a balance-0 cut fact flowBracketBalance tokens off (off+e.length+1) = 0. In the metric-keyed SIBLING seqWindowRecSeqBody this fact (h_bal_m1) falls out of the dispatch FOR FREE — it LOCATES the separator m by balance lo m = 0 then adds the comma's delta-0. The length-arithmetic locator makes no such call. R364's answer: re-source it STRUCTURALLY — the head entry is a complete RecSeqEntry (pbalance e = 0 via toWellBracketed), the .flowEntry separator has delta 0, so e ++ [fe] is balanced; and that derivation is NOT new — it is the h_bal_sep block already proven inline inside the metric-keyed sibling recseqbody_advance (NonemptyStructure.lean:1169). The brick nestedSeq_recseqentry_locate_advance_balance (R364, SeqInteriorSeparators.lean, sorry-free, build green 620 jobs, frontier 4) is that block lifted standalone, keyed on the locator's slice frame — the last non-mechanical dispatch piece before the closed recursion.     • PositionalMapPairDescent — Reflection 509 (the first CONSUMER of R508 ([[CrossFrameStructuralDescent]]) and its POSITIONAL dual: recmappair_window_descent, the inverse of the build-UP recmappair_window.     • BalanceInvisibleSplitGrammarLocated — Reflection 523 (brick (2): mapPairSkeleton_locate, the map analog of matchingClose_full_seq but for the pair's value separator — it PRODUCES the h_skeleton ∃ kv e, … that recmapentry_pair_located (R282/R284) had to take SUPPLIED, from the per-window MapBodyProps [[ThreadingFormVsAssembleForm]] now delivers. Two findings. (1) Balance-invisibility blocks a balance LOCATOR, not the location. A pair .key K .value V returns balance to 0 at the depth-0 .value AND at every nested-entry end, so the .value is balance-invisible (R282) — there is no balance-pure kv locator. But kv is read off the typed grammar: M1 .key head, M3 content-start at lo+1, dispatched by shape into M4 (scalar key ⟹ kv = lo+2) or M5 (bracket key ⟹ kv = j+1, the matching close). The pair end e is the SHARED half — least depth-0 marker via the axis-agnostic firstEntryBoundary — carrying balance lo e = 0 + the marker disjunct + minimality verbatim. (2) The crux kv < e (value separator precedes the first boundary) has a proof cost that BIFURCATES by the key's head shape. Both rule out a marker in (lo, kv]. For a FLAT (scalar) key the two interior positions fall to TOKEN IDENTITY ALONE (lo+1 a scalar, kv the .value; neither .flowEntry, neither hi) — zero balance reasoning. For a NESTED (bracket) key the interior [lo+2, j] is genuinely depth-positive, so it is ruled out by the M5 positivity lifted through lo (balance lo k = 1 + balance (lo+2) k ≥ 1 ≠ 0) — one balance step the flat case never pays; endpoints still fall to token identity. So balance-invisibility ROUTES the location through the grammar, and the head's interior depth profile sets the proof cost. Real lemma audits [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound] (Classical.choice from firstEntryBoundary/flowBracketBalance_compose) — NO sorryAx: consumes only firstEntryBoundary, the MapBodyProps projections, and balance algebra, never the tainted structure lemma ([[MirrorInheritsDependencyAxioms]]).     Navigators, locators & drivers (Reflections 321, 323, 333–338, 350, 351, 353–355, 357–359, 511, 528–532, 534, 535) #

    • PushBlindFramePreserveDependent — Reflection 321 (an accumulator-guard threaded down a recursion has TWO edges with OPPOSITE parent-dependence — the toy of SeqEnclosed and its edges seqEnclosed_descend / seqEnclosed_advance. The guard reads the TOP of an accumulator stack (Enclosed s := s.bind (·.head?) = some true, toy of the enclosing btFold-top fact). DESCEND = PUSH (push b s := s.map (b :: ·), toy of btStep on an opener): pushing a new frame OVERWRITES the top, so the edge is parent-value-BLIND — descend_push needs only ∃ l, s = some l (the parent is DEFINED), NOT that the parent is Enclosed. ADVANCE = FRAME (frame s := s, toy of WellTyped_frame: a balanced segment returns the accumulator unchanged): the top is PRESERVED, so the edge is parent-value-DEPENDENT — advance_frame needs the parent Enclosed and carries it forward. PROVEN nugget: descend_push (from definedness alone, rfl after obtain) and advance_frame (:= h, the frame preserves). POSITIVE/NEGATIVE (#guard) exhibiting the asymmetry: over the SAME non-enclosed parent some [false] (top false), the PUSH edge ENCLOSES (isEnclosed (push true (some [false])) = true — blind to the parent) while the FRAME edge does NOT (isEnclosed (frame (some [false])) = false — cannot manufacture enclosure), so isEnclosed (push true (some [false])) != isEnclosed (frame (some [false])); and isEnclosed (push true none) = false shows DESCEND still needs DEFINEDNESS. Distinct from [[ref-converse-forward-invariant-asymmetry]] (property vs CONVERSE); the accumulator-guard analogue of R288's GuardEdgeFloorAsymmetry (balance guard's edges need different FLOORS — here the split is whether the parent's value is read at all) ([[ref-push-blind-frame-dependent]]))
    • VacuousPositiveCannotExclude — Reflection 323 (a guard/carrier fact of shape prem → pos proven VACUOUSLY — its premise refuted internally — gives the consumer ONLY pos, never ¬ prem, so it CANNOT exclude prem downstream when pos is consistent with the prem-case; thread a DISCRIMINATOR that directly contradicts pos. The toy of seqWindowRecSeqBody's no-trailing-separator obligation m + 1 < hi: the carrier's noTrailingSepFact is (window ends on a depth-0 separator) → isFlowContentStart tokens[hi], proven vacuously (no trailing comma), but its conclusion is CONSISTENT with the trailing-comma window the three-conjunct G genuinely admits — so the carrier alone cannot rule out m + 1 = hi.     • ComplementGuardNotSufficient — Reflection 333 (the COMPLEMENT of a sufficient dispatch guard need not be a sufficient guard — the toy of recseqbody_descend, the DESCEND arm. A spine-walk splits into ADVANCE (p past the head entry) and DESCEND (p inside it). The ADVANCE guard bal lo p = 0 ∧ lo < p is SUFFICIENT for "past the head"; the NAIVE negation bal lo p ≥ 1 is NOT sufficient for "inside the head" — p may be nested in a LATER entry (floor-blind, [[ref-floor-blind-gate-admits-cross-sibling]] on the complement side). The real DESCEND guard is the window-absolute FLOOR ∀ i ∈ (lo,p], bal lo i ≥ 1. Witness 1 , [ [ 2 ] ] (interior of [1,[[2]]]): a SCALAR head 1 (span [0,1)), then a doubly-nested seq. NEGATIVE (#guard): bal 0 3 ≥ 1 holds so the negated guard fires, yet p = 3 ≥ headEnd = 1 (past the head) and the FLOOR fails — bal 0 1 = 0 (the head's return-to-0); the floor is the discriminator the bare ≥ 1 lacks. NEGATIVE (#guard): delta op = delta mop — a seq opener and a map opener share delta +1, so head-is-.seq is provably NOT a balance fact and enters as the dispatch hypothesis h_lo_open, not a derivation. POSITIVE (proven floor_implies_in_head, the crux of recseqbody_descend's h_p_lt): abstractly over any B, if the head balances to 0 at its end m (B m = 0, lo < m) and the floor holds over (lo,p], then p < m — the floor at i = m would force B m ≥ 1, contra = 0. The guard/complement analogue of [[ref-converse-forward-invariant-asymmetry]]; applies [[ref-window-absolute-gate-subset-restriction]] / [[ref-minimal-pair-extracts-the-gate]])
    • AdvanceArmBalanceFree — Reflection 334 (the GENERAL ADVANCE arm is BALANCE-FREE once the caller supplies the decomposition — the toy of recseqbody_advance_general. R334 finding: the balance was never intrinsic to advancing — the CALLER already extracts the head entry and dispatches on p ≥ lo + e.length, so it already holds that inequality AND the cons decomposition body = head ++ sep :: rest; pass them DOWN and the balance machinery EVAPORATES, leaving pure slice algebra + one opener-vs-separator inequality. POSITIVE (proven advance_rebase): GIVEN head ++ sep :: rest, the re-base (head ++ sep :: rest).drop (headLen + 1) = rest is pure List.drop_append — no balance, no floor. POSITIVE (proven sep_ne_opener): if body[headLen]? = some sep, body[p]? = some opener, sep ≠ opener, then p ≠ headLen.     • SeveredEdgeBoundsNavigator — Reflection 335 (a SEVERED recursion edge bounds a navigator's domain; a PATH-blind gate admits windows past it — the toy of the SeqMapPathNestedProbe de-risk that REFUTED rec_seq_body_nested_project's totality.     • AggregateCollapsesStructuredSeparates — Reflection 336 (the dispatch is the un-aggregated STRUCTURED STATE, not a numeric aggregate of it — the toy of SeqPathDispatchProbe.     • ProducerStopsAtSeveredEdge — Reflection 338 (a PRODUCER stops at a severed edge as a leaf; a NAVIGATOR dies there — the toy behind rec_seq_body_nested_project's one-line landing.     • NavigatorDispatchArithmetic — Reflection 350 (a position-indexed navigator splits DECISION from CORRECTNESS: the bottom-up locator's branch dispatch is pure offset/length arithmetic, balance is demoted to the correctness side — the PROBE that settled (i'-b-B2c-nested-fbc-emission-locator), move_trichotomy/move_exclusive pure omega, #guarded on three witnesses in Tests/Guards/Proofs/SeqNestedEntryLocateProbe.lean. R349 left the emission-spine-walk LOCATOR as the residual; the probe asks its single-step shape — (a) one offset-tracked body.length recursion, or (b) a separate balance-locate front end. FINDING (option a): a navigator over a recursive deliverable splits into a DECISION procedure (which branch) and a CORRECTNESS justification (why); the decision is the cheap part. Walking RecSeqBody body with base offset off, the head entry e occupies [off, off+e.length-1], so the target window start a selects the move by comparing a against off+1 and off+e.length ALONE — leaf (a=off+1) / descend (off+1<a<off+e.length, off'=off+1) / advance (off+e.length<a, off'=off+e.length+1), a=off+e.length the impossible separator/close (navMove/classifyMove total, classify_leaf/_descend/_advance/move_exhaustive/move_exclusive pure omega). The structure STORES each entry's length and the offset-slice invariant body=(take H).drop off ties it to absolute coordinates, so the navigator NEVER calls balance to decide. BALANCE IS DEMOTED to correctness: R330's TOP-DOWN projection selected the entry by flowBracketBalance=0 (it had a token coordinate, had to FIND the entry); this BOTTOM-UP locator keyed on [a,b) locates by offset arithmetic, balance entering only the PROOF that the slice equals the window (via all-seq-path/WellTyped), never the selector (topDown_needs_balance vs bottomUp_decides_by_arithmetic). THE RULE: before importing a locate's heavy front end into a navigator descending the same structure, ask which part is the DECISION vs the CORRECTNESS — if the deliverable stores the positional data the decision needs, the decision is a length-arithmetic trichotomy (omega) and the front end belongs only to correctness. Sharpens [[ref-backward-locator-mirrors-forward]] / [[ref-near-leaf-mirror-sheds-machinery]] (the backward/bottom-up direction sheds the forward locate's machinery) and [[ref-from-located-assembler-direction]] (the bottom-up single-step shape); the machinery was already paid for by the structure's stored lengths [[ref-metric-bridge-is-composition]])
    • LeafProductionIsPrefixSlice — Reflection 351 (the LEAF — the only deliverable-PRODUCING move of a position-navigator — is balance-FREE even on the CORRECTNESS side: its window identity is a pure prefix-slice fact; balance is confined to the non-producing interior MOVES. The AUTHORING result that landed the leaf of nestedSeq_recseqentry_locate ((i'-b-B2c-nested-fbc-emission-locator-author)), in NonemptyStructure.lean (head_entry_slice + nestedSeq_recseqentry_locate_leaf, sorry-free, verified-but-unconsumed). R350 split a navigator into DECISION (arithmetic) + CORRECTNESS (balance) and demoted balance to correctness; AUTHORING the leaf SHARPENS that — R350's "correctness needs balance" is too coarse. The leaf, the SINGLE move that produces the deliverable (descend/advance only re-base and recurse), has a correctness obligation — the window identity (L.take (off+e.length)).drop off = e — that is ITSELF balance-free: a pure take/drop prefix-slice fact derivable from the offset-slice invariant (L.take H).drop off = e ++ rest + fit bound off+e.length ≤ H via take_take/drop_take/take_append_of_le_length (head_entry_slice, PROBED axiom-clean before authoring). So at the leaf BOTH halves of R350's split are cheap: decision = length arithmetic (leaf_decision_is_arithmetic), correctness = prefix slicing (leaf_correctness_balance_free); importing balance is dead (leaf_with_balance_redundant). BALANCE IS CONFINED to the non-producing moves: only DESCEND/ADVANCE (which-child navigation) can use it, and they produce NOTHING — the producing move and the balance-using moves are DISJOINT (producing_is_balance_free: produces m → ¬ usesBalance m). "The producing case is the easy case." THE RULE: when a navigator stores children as prefixes/segments and carries an offset-slice invariant, AUTHOR THE LEAF FIRST and expect its correctness to be PURE SLICE ALGEBRA, not the metric the forward locate used; reaching for balance to justify the leaf's output is over-engineering. Sharpens [[ref-navigator-dispatch-is-arithmetic]] (R350: locates WHERE balance enters — not even the leaf's correctness, only the interior moves) and [[ref-near-leaf-mirror-sheds-machinery]] (the leaf sheds even the correctness-side metric); the slice fact is the window identity [[ref-from-located-assembler-direction]]'s assembler consumes)
    • WrapperCarriesInvariant — Reflection 353 (the WRAPPER of a position-navigator is NOT among its mechanical slice bricks: partition the moves by what each PRODUCES, not by step-list position. The R352 de-risk batched "descend + advance + wrapper" as mechanical bricks; authoring descend + advance (nestedSeq_recseqentry_locate_descend/_advance, sorry-free) confirmed the SLICE half but exposed the wrapper's mis-binning. The leaf/descend/advance SLICE bricks re-base coordinates and produce NOTHING (slice_bricks_produce_nothing); the WRAPPER produces the located deliverable (wrapper_produces_and_is_not_a_brick) by threading a DOMAIN INVARIANT + a termination measure. THE TELL: its DESCEND arm (firing at off+1 < a < off+entryLen) excludes scalars/empties by ARITHMETIC alone (scalar_empty_excluded_by_arithmetic — empty descend range), but a MAP head SURVIVES the pure-arithmetic dispatch (arithmetic_dispatch_admits_map — entryLen ≥ 4) yet descending into a map interior is wrong. Arithmetic CANNOT separate a seq head from a map head (arithmetic_cannot_separate_seq_from_map — both survive); only the domain invariant SeqPathAllSeq does (invariant_separates_seq_from_map, via seqPathAllSeq_map_push_breaks). So the wrapper is the invariant-bearing assembly, needing its own interface-design probe before authoring — reaching for it as a "grind" after the slice bricks is the mis-binning. Sharpens [[ref-read-ahead-consumer-chain-proof-state]] (binning by step-LIST position mis-classifies the invariant-bearing assembly as a brick) and complements [[ref-leaf-production-is-prefix-slice]] (R351: the leaf produces nothing → slice algebra; the wrapper is its complement — produces everything, carries the invariant); interface discipline [[ref-from-located-assembler-direction]])
    • VestigialProjectionReopens — Reflection 354 (a VESTIGIAL projection re-opens for a structure-walking consumer: a whole-structure invariant retired as vestigial for a value-blind consumer — which reads only a TOP projection — is LOAD-BEARING for a sibling consumer that WALKS the typed structure. Vestigial-ness is CONSUMER-RELATIVE. The wrapper of nestedSeq_recseqentry_locate (R353) must carry a domain invariant; R354's PROBE (SeqNestedWrapperIfaceProbe.lean, #guard-green on real tokens) settles WHICH: the WHOLE-path SeqPathAllSeq, not the TOP SeqEnclosed/SeqTypedInterior the desc consumer hands it. MINIMAL PAIR — the SAME nested seq [1,2] reached two ways: [[1,2]] (all-[ path, stack [true,true]) vs [{x:[1,2]}] (through a {, stack [true,false,true]). The negative's TOP is true (pos_both/neg_top_holds_full_fails) so it PASSES the projection, but its WHOLE path is not all-true so it FAILS the full invariant — projection_equal_full_separates: the projection canNOT separate the pair, the full invariant DOES. The value-blind consumer ADMITS the bad window (value_blind_admits_bad_window) the structure-walker must REJECT (walker_rejects_bad_window) — same invariant, opposite verdicts. And the bridge "projection ⟹ full" is FALSE (projection_does_not_imply_full, the negative is its counterexample), so the walker sources the full invariant from its OWN descent discipline (descends only through seq openers — R338), not from the projection-providing gate.     • NavigatorArmsKeyedToPositions — Reflection 355 (a position-navigator's per-arm exclusion facts live at DIFFERENT positions: the DESCEND arm's DOMAIN at the recursion BASE off, the LEAF arm's head CLASSIFICATION at the TARGET WINDOW a = off+1. R354 settled the carried domain is the WHOLE-path SeqPathAllSeq tokens off; the blueprint then claimed the interface RESOLVED and attributed the LEAF arm's head-is-.seq to that SAME carried hypothesis. R355's PROBE (SeqNestedLeafEnclosureProbe.lean, #guard-green on real tokens) finds the attribution IMPRECISE and the carried-state list INCOMPLETE: SeqPathAllSeq tokens off is BLIND to tokens[off] (the head entry's own opener — it constrains only the stack BEFORE off), so it cannot decide whether the head is a [ (seq, leaf admissible) or { (map, leaf inadmissible). MINIMAL PAIR — SAME body base off = 2, SAME would-be leaf window a = 3, differing only in the head bracket TYPE: [[1,2]] (seq head, SeqEnclosed 3 = true) vs [{x:[1,2]}] (map head, SeqEnclosed 3 = false).     • CarriedInvariantAdvanceEdge — Reflection 357 (a recursion that CARRIES a domain invariant needs one PRESERVATION edge per recursion MOVE — descend AND advance — not per deliverable constructor; the descend edge + its NEGATION mask the missing ADVANCE (frame) edge.     • JointNavigatorDriverCrossDeliverable — Reflection 529 (the JOINT map+seq body navigator driver recbody_joint_navigator_driver, the brick resolving the cross-deliverable IH knot the two single drivers (R510/R528) left open.     • JointContentPackFromCarrier — Reflection 531 (the JOINT per-window CONTENT-PACK provider recbody_joint_content_pack — the carrier→content feed for the joint descend [[JointDescendTailDispatch]] R530.     • BoundaryInclusiveSiblingCascade — Reflection 471 (the BOUNDARY-INCLUSIVE SIBLING CASCADE: when a BUNDLED multi-structure deliverable needs the opener-inclusive convention, EACH structure owes its own opener-inclusive sibling, but the COST is GRADED and the boundary token INVERTS which field is hardest. Toy: consumer_erases (projects P from P ∧ Q, the erasure); reexport the structural heart (re-runs the dependency keeping Q, applies the sibling to recover R); erasure_is_genuine the negative (R is NOT recoverable from ∃ w, P w alone — P:=True, Q:=R:=False admits the consumer's ∃ w, P w yet ∃ w, P w ∧ R w is FALSE, so re-export is NECESSARY); sibling_le/reexport_concrete the by-name, placement-forcing variant; two concrete non-vacuous runs. Sharpens [[ref-reduction-by-import]] (the dependency already computed the fact; the consumer threw it away — re-run, don't re-derive) and [[ref-additive-parallel-type-over-shared-edit]] (the re-export is a sibling, not an in-place return-type edit))
    • WeakFamilyMemberProjectsDepthFree — Reflection 479 (the WEAK member of a child-window structure family PROJECTS its strong sibling through the family's existing strong→weak projector, residuals VACUOUS against the window's own interior floor — and is DEPTH-AGNOSTIC because the floor is keyed on the CHILD origin. The brick flowBodyContent_child_bracket landed this turn — the (α.2) content half of the seq enclosingLocate residual, the THIRD and final opener-inclusive child-bracket sibling completing the trio {flowBodyWindow_child_bracket, flowBodyContentDeep_child_bracket, flowBodyContent_child_bracket}. FlowBodyContent is a PROJECTION of FlowBodyContentDeep (the family ships the projector flowBodyContent_of_deep), so the weak member is NOT a fresh producer: pipe the all-depth deep sibling (flowBodyContentDeep_child_bracket) through that projector and discharge its two residuals (bodySucc, noTrailingSep) VACUOUSLY — the only child-origin balance-0 prefix ends in [k, j+1) are the two ENDPOINTS (the opener sits at balance ≥ 1 by the floor so its =0 premise fails; the close takes the trivial left disjunct and contradicts the .flowEntry premise via its own .flowSequenceEnd type). DEPTH-AGNOSTIC: the strict floor is keyed on the CHILD origin k (balance k i ≥ 1), so NO balance lo k = 0 discriminator appears — the depth caveat lives ENTIRELY in the LOCATING sibling's outer-origin scan, the family splitting into a depth-NEEDING locator and a depth-FREE projector. Toy: Floor/Content/content_of_deep model the family + projector; content_child_bracket the structural heart (pipe deep through the projector, both residuals from the k-keyed floor + close type); floor_is_load_bearing the negative (drop the floor and an INTERIOR balance-0 at i=2 breaks bodySucc, demanding 1 = 3); a concrete non-vacuous run. Realizes the projection flowBodyContentDeep_child_bracket's doc only PROMISED; instance of [[ref-complete-projection-family-for-new-member]] (the content member's PROJECTION obligation) reusing the projector per [[ref-coerce-to-weaker-reuse-wrapper]], residuals discharged as [[ref-boundary-residual-end-dual]] vacuous boundary facts)
    • ScanOwnsTheDepthDebt — Reflection 480 (the depth-0 premise belongs to the LOCATING SCAN, not to any family member: take the located close as INPUT and EVERY child-window constructor is DEPTH-AGNOSTIC — a sharpening of R479. The brick flowBodyWindow_child_bracket_at landed this turn — the WINDOW member of the "given close" child-bracket trio, completing {flowBodyWindow_child_bracket_at, flowBodyContentDeep_child_bracket, flowBodyContent_child_bracket} for the (α) enclosingLocate assemble. flowBodyWindow_child_bracket LOCATES the matching close j via the forward scan flowBracketBalance_matching_close, and THAT scan is the SOLE reason it needs the depth-0 discriminator balance lo k = 0 (the scan reads the close off the lo-keyed balance returning to depth 0, which coincides with the bracket's own close only at depth 0).     • GenericDiscriminantUnderdeterminesOutput — Reflection 481 (a GENERIC-discriminant premise underdetermines a TYPE-SPECIFIC output — surface the refinement as an explicit hypothesis, don't derive it. Discovered while landing the brick seqEnclosingLocate_of_seqOpener_at_depth this turn — the (α) enclosingLocate ASSEMBLE for the seq child, feeding ONE located close UNIFORMLY to all three "given close" child-bracket constructors (R479/R480) to deliver lo ≤ p ∧ ∃ hiE, b ≤ hiE ∧ hiE ≤ size ∧ hiE ≤ hi ∧ FlowBodyWindow/Deep/Content tokens p hiE. seqWidthEnc_of_enclosingLocate_and_recIH (R475) hands the assemble the GENERIC located-opener premise flowBracketDelta tokens[p]!.val = 1 ("p is some opener") — but delta = 1 admits BOTH .flowSequenceStart ([) and .flowMappingStart ({), while the seq output FlowBodyContent/the close locator (R478) force a [. The assemble's job is to SURFACE the irreducible refinement as a named consume-side residual, not invent a derivation that cannot exist. The five moves: (1) lo ≤ p (R476), (2) locate close hiS (R478), (3) child-keyed floor balance p i ≥ 1 by one compose through the opener, (4) hiS < hi from balance lo hiS = 1 ≠ 0 = balance lo hi, (5) the three depth-free constructors at k := p, j := hiS, hiE := hiS + 1. Same shape as the head-blind/end-free gate findings ([[ref-probe-provider-head-blind-gate]], [[ref-end-free-gate-underdetermines-close]]) but on the CONSUMER's supplied premise, not the producer's precondition; the assemble pays the family's depth once at the locator per [[ref-scan-owns-the-depth-debt]])
    • DepthGeneralMatchingClose — Reflection 482 (the located encloser is at depth ≥ 1, so the close locator must be DEPTH-GENERAL; the matching-close scan is purely RELATIVE, so the depth-0 lemma re-bases mechanically. Discovered while trying to WIRE the R481 seq assemble seqEnclosingLocate_of_seqOpener_at_depth into seqWidthEnc_of_enclosingLocate_and_recIH (R475): the seq carrier seqLocalCarrier_of_widthEnc locates each gated window's INNERMOST enclosing opener p (seqEnclosingOpener_of_gate) and asks enclosingLocate for p's span [p, hiE). (The OTHER discriminator h_open : .flowSequenceStart is FREE in the consume context — seqOpenerType_of_located_and_gate derives it from the gate's enclosure mark; only h_p_depth was the gap, and it is not even true.) Toy: matchesClose B k j states the close relation as differences from B k; matchesClose_shift proves it INVARIANT under a uniform shift B' = B + c (the formal content of "depth-relative, so depth-0 re-bases to any depth"); depth0_precondition_fails the obstruction (Bnest 1 = 1 ≠ 0 — the nested opener); nested_opener_has_matching_close (matchesClose Bnest 1 3 — the close IS locatable at depth 1). The shift does NOT transfer the global floor (B i ≥ 0 ↦ B' i ≥ c), which is why the real proof re-proves rather than reduces. Continues [[ref-scan-owns-the-depth-debt]] (R480 located the depth debt in the scan; this pays it by generalizing the scan) via [[ref-rebase-fact-from-enclosing-window]])
    • NestedTypedLocatorIsAFrame — Reflection 483 (the nested TYPED close locator is a FRAME, not a re-proof — and the frame is CLEANER than the hand-rolled depth-0 special case. The typed half of the R482 obstruction: seqEnclosingOpener_of_gate returns a depth-≥1 innermost opener, so the typed locators' h_k_depth : balance lo k = 0 is FALSE too. R482 fixed the BALANCE core by RE-BASING (a scalar shifts uniformly under nesting, so the proof re-derives against balance lo k, since the global Dyck floor does not shift-transfer). R483 fixes the TYPED layer by FRAMING: the nested opener sits over a base stack s_k whose bottom d markers are INERT across the positive-depth span, so the depth-0 argument frames by s_k — opener pushes b :: s_k (btStep_frame), the balanced WellTyped interior folds from b :: s_k back to b :: s_k (WellTyped_frame, replacing the depth-0 proof's bespoke btFold_getLast?_preserved), and btStep_frame_inv strips s_k off the close — collapsing the nested btStep tokens[j]! The dividing line: RE-BASE when the carrier is a scalar difference; FRAME when it is a stack with an inert suffix. Landed (verified-but-unconsumed, axioms [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound], frontier still 4): matching_close_typed_{generic,core}_nested + flowBracketBalance_matching_close_{seq,map}_nested in WellBracketed.lean part 5.5 — all DROP h_k_depth and take the relative interior floor the balance core outputs (so the seq/map wrappers are simpler than their depth-0 originals — no flowBracketBalance_interior_dyck re-derivation). Toy: step/fold stack machine, step_frame/fold_frame the two framing engines, fold_frame_self the "balanced body returns to ANY prefix" (WellTyped_frame analogue), nested_close_via_frame the punchline (the nested close IS the depth-0 close framed by s_k), and nestedWord/inner_close_is_framed a concrete depth-1 [ { x } ] witness. Contrast DepthGeneralMatchingClose (R482, the BALANCE re-base) — same obstruction, complementary repair shapes)
    • DepthHypCostIsItsEqualityReads — Reflection 484 (a base-depth hypothesis h_p_depth : balance lo p = 0 costs exactly its EQUALITY reads to drop — countable by grep before editing. The CONSUME side of R483: once the depth-general PRIMITIVE exists (flowBracketBalance_matching_close_seq_nested, [[NestedTypedLocatorIsAFrame]]), re-proving a consumer without h_p_depth is scoped by tracing where the hypothesis is READ. Two kinds: a FLOOR read (a fact from the Dyck floor ≥ 0, monotone/frame-stable — never touches h_p_depth, free at every depth) vs an EQUALITY read (an rw [h_depth] substituting the pinned = 0 — depth-BOUND, gone at depth ≥ 1, the downstream omega must re-derive from ≥ 0). Cost(drop) = (locator call → pure SWAP to the depth-general twin) + (one re-derivation per =0-equality read). R484 landed seqClose_of_located_and_enclosing_within_nested (SeqInteriorSeparators.lean): it has ZERO equality reads — every downstream fact (a ≤ j, balance a j = 0, b ≤ j, upper containment j ≤ hi via seqLocatedClose_within_body) is a floor read off the enclosing ≥ 0, so dropping h_p_depth is a pure swap (…_seq → …_seq_nested), body byte-identical, axioms [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound], frontier still 4. The SIBLING R481 seqEnclosingLocate_of_seqOpener_at_depth has exactly ONE equality read — step (4)'s rw [h_p_depth] at hc1 computing balance lo hiS = 0 + 1 = 1; dropping it there costs swapping to balance lo hiS = d + 1 ≥ 1 > 0 from d ≥ 0 (the next brick). Toy: closeN/close0 (the depth-general locator subsumes its d = 0 special case), consumerA0/consumerAN the pure-swap pair (floor-only reads, byte-identical bodies), consumerB0/consumerBN the equality-read pair (the rw [h] becomes one omega re-derivation), and consumer{A,B}_general_subsumes witnessing the general consumers reproduce the depth-0 ones. Pairs with [[NestedTypedLocatorIsAFrame]] (the primitive) and [[ScanOwnsTheDepthDebt]])
    • SurfacedResidualFreeAtWiring — Reflection 486 (a refinement surfaced as an explicit consume-side residual is often discharged FOR FREE at the wiring site from a CO-LOCATED invariant the consumer already carries — the POSITIVE closure of R481 GenericDiscriminantUnderdeterminesOutput. R481 proved the seq assemble's tokens[p]!.val = .flowSequenceStart is NOT derivable from the generic slot premise flowBracketDelta tokens[p]!.val = 1 (a { satisfies delta = 1 yet has a map child), so it SURFACED the refinement as an explicit hypothesis h_open. The discriminating question is not "does the producer's NAMED premise determine the refinement?" (R481: no) but "does SOME fact co-located at the wiring determine it?" (R486: yes — the gate mark); a residual is only truly owed by the caller if NO co-located invariant pins it.     Mirrors, duals & axis transport #

    Mirror cost & verbatim transport (Reflections 309, 310, 360, 362, 363, 366, 516, 594, 595) #

    • BackwardLocatorMirrorsForward — Reflection 309 (a backward locator mirrored from a forward one needs NO standalone backward-matching-open primitive, and its DELIVERABLE is sized to the consumer's reads — the toy of flowBracketBalance_backward_open_locate, the HARD CORE of the (i'-b-backward-locator) descent. Tokens are abstracted to a delta stream d : Nat → Int (each delta in {-1,0,1}); bal d n is the running balance from 0. Correction 1 (POSITIVE backwardLocate): the locator ∃ p, p < a ∧ d p = 1 ∧ bal d (p+1) = bal d a is proved by Nat.strongRecOn on a, casing the last delta d (a-1) into opener/neutral/closer; the closer case does NOT author a standalone backward-matching-open lemma (the mirror of a forward matching-close scan) — the IH at a-1 IS the closer's matching opener p', and a one-line pairSkip (bal d a = bal d p', toy of flowBracketBalance_bracket_pair_skip) jumps the matched block, then the IH at p' walks outward. The recursion structure IS the backward matching-open; mirroring a locator backward SHEDS machinery ([[ref-near-leaf-mirror-sheds-machinery]] on the matched-block-skip axis), it does not add it. Correction 2 (NEGATIVE, #guard-backed): the deliverable carries NO Dyck FLOOR, even though the real forward lemma has a symmetric one — the consumer (Located/the FROM-LOCATED assembler) reads only loS := p+1, loS ≤ a, bal d loS = bal d a (locatorAssembles builds it in one line). On [ ] [ (dd = [1,-1,1], bal dd 3 = 1) the pruned existential is satisfied by BOTH the true innermost opener p=2 AND a SPURIOUS outer opener p=0 whose floor dips negative — #guard floorOK dd 3 3 == true vs #guard floorOK dd 1 3 == false; the floor would separate them, but the consumer needs neither innermost-ness nor the floor, only bal d loS = bal d a, so dropping the floor (≈ half the proof: the closer case's floor-extension across the skipped block) loses nothing the assembler consumes. The locator's deliverable shape is set by what the assembler READS ([[ref-from-located-assembler-direction]]: the assembler's hypotheses ARE the locator's exact contract), not by the forward lemma's symmetric conclusion-set. The de-risk was honestly pessimistic — the feared new infrastructure never materialized — so authoring is the real test of a de-risk's hardness prediction, sharpening [[ref-probe-deferred-universal-before-producing]])
    • MetricBridgeIsComposition — Reflection 310 (a feared "bridge between two PARALLEL metrics" is often ALREADY a theorem: a STRUCTURAL count and a NUMERIC count that share a substrate are equated by COMPOSITION, not a new per-step induction — the toy of flowBracketBalance_pos_of_btFold_head, the gate→hypothesis bridge of (i'-b-locator-glue-gate-bridge). A token stream (op b pushes a Bool, cl pops, neu neutral) carries two metrics: the NUMERIC bal (fold of {+1, -1, 0}) and the STRUCTURAL fold (a List Bool, its LENGTH the metric). The de-risk feared they might DIVERGE (push map vs seq differently), needing a per-step induction relating pushes to deltas. Correction 1 (POSITIVE bridge): they do not — both reduce to the SAME substrate bal via foldLen (stack length = bal), so bridge (non-empty stack ⇒ bal ≥ 1) is foldLen + "non-empty ⇒ length ≥ 1" + omega; the per-step reconciliation the de-risk feared IS foldLen, which already exists. Type-agnostic dividend (POSITIVE): bridge takes = some hd for an ARBITRARY hd : Bool — the proof is blind to the head value, so ONE lemma serves both axes (bridgeTrue/bridgeFalse, seq/map). Correction 2 (NEGATIVE, #guard-backed): the fear is legitimate IN GENERAL — a WRONG metric wfold (pushes only on op true, ignoring op false) genuinely diverges: on diverge = [op false, op true], #guard (fold …).map (·.length) == some 2 and bal == 2 (real metrics coincide) but #guard (wfold …).map (·.length) == some 1 (≠ bal). So VERIFY the substrate is shared (every op _ pushes uniformly AND contributes +1); don't assume divergence (author an induction) NOR coincidence (skip the check). Sharpens [[ref-probe-deferred-universal-before-producing]] on the metric-unification axis, as [[ref-backward-locator-mirrors-forward]] did on the machinery axis)
    • RefuteArmMirrorsPinningSibling — Reflection 360 (a flagged "needs-new-infra" REFUTATION arm is often a PINNING sibling's MIRROR: flip the type-discriminator bit, relax the pin-hypothesis to a floor. The R359 audit deferred the DESCEND map-head arm as needing a not-yet-existing map-frame-persists lemma; authoring it (testing that pessimism, [[ref-backward-locator-mirrors-forward]]) showed it was NOT new infra. seqPathAllSeq_map_frame_persists (SeqInteriorSeparators.lean, R360, sorry-free, build green 617 jobs, frontier 4) is a near-twin of the EXISTING sibling seqOpenerType_of_located_and_gate, which PINS the located opener's type (tokens[p] = .flowSequenceStart). Both read the SAME substrate btFold_frame_inv (a floored fold from a base whose bottom frame is bit :: base returns m ++ (bit :: base) — the frame survives). The refutation is the mirror with TWO edits: (1) FLIP the bit true→false (a [→{) and conclude the OPPOSITE membership (a surviving false ⟹ not all-true); (2) RELAX the pin-hypothesis — pinning needs the EXACT body balance (forces the surviving prefix m = [] so the head is read directly), refuting needs only the FLOOR (the frame is never popped, m may be nonempty above the false). The substrate already existed for the adjacent consumer; the "new infra" was a parametric twin. Probed true on [{a: [1]}] (every position strictly inside the map span (1,7] carries a false) before authoring. The refuted hypothesis is the window's seq-enclosure SeqPathAllSeq tokens (a-1) (R355), making the map-head DESCEND case vacuous (no fifth arm). Toy: shared FrameInv substrate (S = m ++ (bit :: base)), pin sibling (bit true + exact m = [] ⟹ head pinned), refute mirror (bit false, NO exact hyp ⟹ surviving false ⟹ not all-true); #guards show the pin's all-true stack, the mirror's surviving false with a NONEMPTY m, and the NEGATIVE (without the surviving frame, [].all = true — the floor is load-bearing). Sharpens [[ref-compose-arm-seam-before-skeleton]]; builds on [[ref-near-leaf-mirror-sheds-machinery]] + [[ref-backward-locator-mirrors-forward]]; applies [[ref-metric-bridge-is-composition]])
    • TypeBlindBridgeFalse — Reflection 362 (a balance-only invariant is TYPE-BLIND, so a named "structure ⇒ typed-fact" bridge across a type-blind constructor is FALSE; PROBE a minimal pair before authoring, and on refutation THREAD the typed fact as a parametric-assembler hypothesis. Composing the ADVANCE seam nestedSeq_recseqentry_locate_advance_step, the domain brick seqPathAllSeq_advance demanded the consumed segment be WellTyped. The plan named a discharge — "the head entry is a RecSeqEntry, a balanced bracket pair ⇒ WellTyped" — and flagged SMALLEST-FIRST to probe it. The probe REFUTED it: WellBracketed (the pbalance Dyck condition) is type-blind because flowBracketDelta maps BOTH [/{ to +1 and BOTH closers to −1, so it cannot tell bracket TYPES apart; and RecSeqEntry.map gates its interior ONLY on WellBracketed, admitting a mistyped [ }. Concretely { [ } } has pbalance = 0 (passes the gate) yet btFold (some []) · = none (NOT WellTyped) — ONE #guard witness refutes the universal bridge. The move on refutation: do NOT strengthen the shared inductive ([[ref-additive-parallel-type-over-shared-edit]]) and do NOT chase a stronger local invariant (a type-blind invariant cannot CREATE a typed fact, only TRANSPORT one — [[ref-type-blind-invariant-transports-via-converse-frame]]); THREAD the typed fact as a parametric-assembler hypothesis ([[ref-parametric-assembler-extraction]]) in structural form WellTyped (e ++ [fe]) (slice bridge proven internally), isolating its production as a later frame-transport. Toy: bal (type-blind balance, both openers +1) vs tfold/typed (a stack fold that PUSHES which bracket type opened and POPs only the match); Struct.wrap gates only on balOnly; #guards show the mistyped { [ } } passes balOnly but fails typed, an example builds it as a real Struct that is provably ¬ typed, and seam threads typed as a hypothesis it cannot manufacture from Struct. Sharpens [[ref-probe-deferred-universal-before-producing]] + [[ref-minimal-pair-extracts-the-gate]] (the probe target is a NAMED bridge refuted by one #guard witness, not a ∀-survey); seam context [[ref-compose-arm-seam-before-skeleton]])
    • DeferredTypedFactInGuard — Reflection 363 (a seam's deferred "source it LATER by frame-transport" typed-fact obligation is often ALREADY carried by the recursion's existing guard G and ALREADY transported by a SIBLING driver over that same G — the de-risk is a READ, not a build. R362 threaded the ADVANCE arm's WellTyped (e ++ [fe]) and filed its production as a residual to be "sourced later by frame-transport from a global WellTyped." Two reads resolved it. (1) The guard: the consuming recursion's four-conjunct G carries FlowBodyWindow tokens off H, and FlowBodyWindow.wellTyped IS the WHOLE-window typed fact (.dyck the floor) — so the "global WellTyped to transport down" is a FIELD of a guard the recursion already descends along, not a fact to manufacture. (2) The sibling: seqWindowRecSeqBody (R323), a driver over the SAME G, had ALREADY written the exact transport at its own depth-0 cut m+1 — WellTyped_subrange … h_win.wellTyped h_bal_m1 (h_win.dyck …) (lines 1860-1863). The new supplier nestedSeq_recseqentry_locate_advance_welltyped (R363, SeqInteriorSeparators.lean, sorry-free, build green 619 jobs, frontier 4) is that four-line transport re-aimed at this seam's slice — same body, different cut. So the ADVANCE arm has TWO landed content pieces (the consume-seam R362 + the produce-supplier R363) and the skeleton owes ZERO content bricks. Toy: Win guard BUNDLES the whole-context typed fact as a field wholeTyped; SubrangeTransporter is the pre-existing transport primitive (modeled as given — re-used, not re-derived); sibling calls it on the guard field at one cut and supplier re-aims the SAME call at the seam's e ++ [fe] (token-identical bodies); POSITIVE = a concrete Win whose field holds and whose seam-prefix is genuinely typed; NEGATIVE = the guard field is load-bearing — on the mistyped { [ } } Win is uninhabitable, so the typed fact must hold UPSTREAM, never manufactured. Sharpens [[ref-type-blind-invariant-transports-via-converse-frame]]; echoes [[ref-metric-bridge-is-composition]] + [[ref-root-seed-recursive-producer-swap]]; discipline [[ref-compose-arm-seam-before-skeleton]])
    • AdjacentOriginReconstructs — Reflection 366 (a consumer fact at an origin ADJACENT to a carried bundle's origin is NOT a separate guard-debt; it reconstructs by single-token compose across the boundary token whose delta the guard already pins — so itemize the consumer's debt as ONE window-absolute bundle, deriving the lower-origin fact in place. R366 landed the LEAF branch of the emission-spine-walk locator's per-window step h_step (nestedSeq_recseqentry_locate_leaf_typed, SeqInteriorSeparators.lean, sorry-free, full build green 621, frontier 4). The LEAF seam nestedSeq_recseqentry_locate_leaf_full (R359) reads TWO balance facts at DIFFERENT origins: h_inner (interior balance flowBracketBalance (off+1) b = 0, origin off+1) and h_floor (the ENCLOSURE floor ∀ i ∈ (off, b], flowBracketBalance off i ≥ 1, origin off, one token LOWER — the opener's own position). A naïve G carries both. But G is keyed window-ABSOLUTE on the FIXED [off+1, b) (R356) via SeqTypedInterior tokens (off+1) b, whose interior floor is ≥ 0 at origin off+1 — no off-origin floor. The origin gap is exactly ONE token, the opener at off whose delta the guard pins (tokens[off]! = [ ⇒ flowBracketDelta = +1), so flowBracketBalance off i = flowBracketBalance off (off+1) + flowBracketBalance (off+1) i = (+1) + (≥0) ≥ 1 (flowBracketBalance_compose + the single-token opener read) — the ≥ 1 floor is DERIVED, NOT a separate field. So G owes the LEAF ONE balance bundle, not two floors. Toy: bal (recursive cumulative balance over per-position deltas δ); bal_step (the single-token compose, toy of flowBracketBalance_compose); floor_lo_from_floor_hi reconstructs the ≥ 1 floor at origin lo from the ≥ 0 floor at lo+1 + the opener pin δ lo = 1. POSITIVE = the reconstruction FIRES on a concrete opener window (δopen, opener at lo=2); NEGATIVE = LOAD-BEARING on the pin — with a delta-0 token at lo (δflat) the interior floor STILL holds but the ≥ 1 enclosure floor FAILS (bal lo (lo+1) = 0), so an unpinned/non-adjacent origin IS a genuine debt. Origin-shift instance of [[ref-reconstruct-in-place-over-relocate]]; keeps fields window-absolute per [[ref-window-absolute-gate-subset-restriction]]; the LEAF de-risk of [[ref-width-recursion-combinator-before-grammar-step]]'s SEARCH step)
    • MirrorInheritsDependencyAxioms — Reflection 516 (a faithful proof-MIRROR inherits its DEPENDENCY's axiom profile, which can differ from its sibling's — the axiom-layer companion to landing brick (2)'s first sub-brick mapWholeStreamWellTyped + mapFoldTotal_of_context (the h_fold_total residual of the map fold mapHRec_of_root_and_emit, the some false/{/} dual of seqWholeStreamWellTyped/seqFoldTotal_of_context). The proofs are a faithful text-swap, yet #print axioms diverges: seqWholeStreamWellTyped is [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound] (clean) while mapWholeStreamWellTyped is [propext, sorryAx, Classical.choice, Quot.sound] — it carries sorryAx. The asymmetry is invisible in the proof TEXT; it comes entirely from the structure-lemma DEPENDENCY: the SEQ structure lemma scanFiltered_emitSeq_nonempty_structure had its FlowSubrangesOk := sorry residual RELOCATED out at R442, but the MAP one scanFiltered_emitMap_nonempty_structure still discharges its ParseEntryFlowMapOk conjunct with that sorry INLINE (NonemptyStructure.lean:11208, frontier sorry #4) — so the WHOLE map context-provider family inherits sorryAx, even though mapWholeStreamWellTyped reads ONLY the clean boundary/WellTyped fields, never the tainted conjunct. Two findings PINNED: (1) axiom inheritance is at CONSTANT-dependency granularity, not field-usage granularity — projecting only the CLEAN field off ⟨clean, tainted⟩ STILL inherits the taint, because #print axioms walks the syntactic dependency closure of every referenced constant and does not prune constructor fields the proof never reduces; so a "pure text-swap mirror" claim must be qualified by auditing the DEPENDENCIES' axioms, not just the proof body; (2) a cleanup that fixed one sibling may never have been mirrored on the other — when two siblings diverge in axiom profile despite identical proofs, look for an asymmetric upstream cleanup (here R442) and either mirror it (a separate relocation brick that would clean the whole map family at once) or state the inheritance honestly. Adds NO NEW sorry — routes through the existing #4 — and the sorryAx vanishes the moment the root carriers close #4; frontier still 4. Demo (self-contained core Lean, no imports) uses a custom axiom relocated_residual standing in for the real sorryAx: a Struct with a clean boundary field and a hard parserOk field proved via the axiom in the TAINTED sibling but trivial-y in the CLEAN one, and two readers seqWhole/mapWhole with BYTE-IDENTICAL bodies ·.boundary; #guard_msgs pins seq_clean axiom-FREE while map_tainted/demo carry [relocated_residual] — though neither touches the hard field. Companion to [[MapRootProjection]] (the prior brick) and [[SeqRootReconciliation]] (R512, whose seq context pair this mirrors); sharpens [[MirrorCostDeltaAlphabet]] / [[MirrorReadsConjunctNotProjection]] by adding the axiom layer to the mirror-cost accounting)
    • ScalarComposeValue — Reflection 594 (standalone scalar parse, composed value pin (SS5.12 continued).
