    Probing, de-risking & refutation #

    Probe before producing (Reflections 297, 303, 313, 339, 340, 468, 612, 614, 615, 616, 617, 618, 622, 625, 638) #

    • MinimalPairExtractsTheGate — Reflection 297 (probe a gated ∀-over-sub-windows as a MINIMAL PAIR; the gate is read off, not invented — the toy of the BodySuccSeqDiscriminator concrete-emitter probe. When a deferred universal is FALSE in general but you suspect a gate rescues it, do not probe a single (holding) instance — probe a minimal pair inside one object: one window that HOLDS the property and a sibling that FAILS it. The object [ { k : v } , x ] (tok/stream) holds a seq interior [1,8) and a map interior [2,5). POSITIVE seq_window_holds ∧ seq_window_is_seq_typed: succHolds 1 8 = true (toy of bodySucc — the nested map is invisible, balance > 0 there) and the gate classifies it enclosingMark 0 = some true. NEGATIVE map_window_fails ∧ map_window_is_map_typed: succHolds 2 5 = false (the key at k=2 is a depth-0 end followed by colon, not a comma separator) and the gate classifies it enclosingMark 1 = some false. The probe does triple duty: confirms the gate SUFFICIENT (holding window), proves it NECESSARY (failing sibling), and NAMES it as the existing typed-stack-top computation (stFold/enclosingMark, toy of WellTyped/btFold: seqOpen ↦ true, mapOpen ↦ false) that SEPARATES the pair. The lesson: the structural feature that differs between the holding and failing windows IS the discriminator — read it off the pair, do not invent it)
    • ProbeDeferredProviderHeadBlindGate — Reflection 303 (probing a deferred provider universal BEFORE producing it catches a FALSE universal: a HEAD-BLIND gate admits spurious windows where the body-structure route is undischargeable — the toy of the de-risk that re-scoped (i'-b-descend-root-provider-descent), behind SafeBodyUnit_head_Q / not_safeBodyUnit_of_head_flowEntry. The planned descent would prove the seq carrier via a provider universal delivering a SafeBodyUnit at EVERY window the gate SeqTypedInterior admits; but the gate (balance-0 ∧ enclosing-seq) is HEAD-BLIND — it never inspects whether the window's first token is a content start — so it ADMITS separator-headed windows, where a SafeBodyUnit is impossible (its head must be a content start). The real minimal pair: on scanFiltered (emit [[1,2],9]) = SS [ [ "1" , "2" ] , "9" ] SE, the two depth-0 commas at indices 4, 7 BOTH satisfy the gate at [a,a+1) yet their [comma] slice is no SafeBodyUnit — so provider 4 5 / provider 7 8 are unprovable. Toy substrate: tokens are Nats (0 = separator, n>0 = content), Gate l := l ≠ [] ∧ (l.map delta).sum = 0 (head-blind — every token is delta-0, mirroring flowEntry/scalar both flowBracketDelta = 0), Body = nonempty content-headed entries separated by single 0s (toy SafeBodyUnit). KERNEL Body_head_content (a Body forces a positive head, toy of SafeBodyUnit_head_Q) and not_Body_of_head_zero (the dead-end guard). MINIMAL PAIR: gate_good ∧ body_good on [1,0,2] (content-headed: gate AND body) vs gate_bad ∧ not_body_bad on [0] (lone separator: gate STILL holds head-blind, but NO body). NECESSARY-BUT-NOT-SUFFICIENT gate_trailing ∧ not_body_trailing on [1,0] — a CONTENT-headed window can still fail Body (trailing separator), so content-start-alignment alone would not rescue the provider. CAPSTONE provider_is_false : ¬ (∀ l, Gate l → Body l) (witnessed at [0]) vs good_window_discharges (the carrier is true where the recursion actually instantiates it). Decidable #guards: the gate accepts [0] and [1,0,2] alike (head-blind), the head differs (1 vs 0) yet [1,0]'s content head still fails Body. The lesson: when a deferred universal will be produced over a gate, #eval-probe a minimal pair FIRST — a head-blind gate that admits a window the deliverable cannot inhabit makes the producer's hypothesis FALSE; catching it before producing converts a planned multi-brick recursion into a retargeted direct discharge, and the false intermediate is fenced off by a proven lemma rather than a discovered sorry)
    • FloorBlindGateAdmitsCrossSibling — Reflection 313 (a FLOOR-BLIND gate — depth-0-balanced only — admits CROSS-SIBLING windows the deliverable cannot inhabit, so the carrier is FALSE on a valid witness; the missing local-Dyck floor excludes EXACTLY those windows and discharges the consumer's containment bound b ≤ hiS for free — the toy of the R313 strengthening of SeqTypedInterior. The forward-CLOSE brick's de-risk found that the gate balance a b = 0 ∧ seq-enclosed admitted the cross-sibling window [3,7) of [[1],[2]] (from inside the first inner seq to inside the second), on which bodySuccFact is FALSE — so SeqInteriorSeparators was false on a valid value. Toy alphabet op/cl/ct (+1/-1/0); witness W = op op ct cl op ct cl cl (skeleton of [[1],[2]]); bal the running balance. NEGATIVE (#guard-backed): window [2,6) has bal 2 6 = 0 (passes the BARE gate) yet DIPS to bal 2 4 = -1 (floor violated) and bodySucc [2,6) is FALSE (ct at 2 is depth-0-complete but W[3] = cl, not a separator); the floor SEPARATES it. floored ⟹ bodySucc at every balanced sub-window (#guard-enumerated), and a genuine entry window [2,3) is floored AND satisfies bodySucc. POSITIVE (proven, floored_window_cannot_cross, [propext, Quot.sound]): a window satisfying the local-Dyck floor cannot cross a close that underflows its balance — bal a (m+1) < 0 contradicts the floor at m+1 — which is exactly the consumer's b ≤ hiS = j for free.     • ProbeRefutesComplementProvider — Reflection 339 (a probe of a would-be COMPLEMENT provider REFUTES its necessity — the toy behind seqEnclosed_of_seqTypedInterior's one-line landing. So StrongPath is VESTIGIAL and ONE producer serves the whole gated domain — the size-down dual of [[ref-downstream-derisk-restores-upstream]] (the discriminator was never needed; the gate carried it))
    • EndFreeGateUnderdeterminesClose — Reflection 340 (an END-FREE gate underdetermines a window's CLOSE — the toy behind the (i'-b-B2c-provider-from-gate) probe SeqGateWindownessProbe. A provider quantified under a gate whose conjuncts constrain only window-INTERNAL invariants (balance, enclosure mark, Dyck floor — none mentioning the token AT the end b) cannot read its structural close off get s b. Toy: Tok := op | cl | it | sep; bal/windowBal over a slice; an END-free Gate with one balance-0 conjunct. PROBE (proven gate_24 + end_24_not_close): the b ≠ close window [2,4) = it sep (mirror of "1" ,) on W = op op it sep it cl sep it cl (mirror of [[1,2],9]) PASSES the gate yet get W 4 = it, NOT cl. SHARPENED by Reflection 356 (gate_admits_three_ends/close_token_separates_three_ends on V = op it sep it sep it cl): a SETTLED next-step pointer (the emission-spine wrapper nestedSeq_recseqentry_locate, R355) re-credited balance-0 with pinning b; re-running the probe shows the gate admits not one but ARBITRARILY MANY non-close ends (a seq of n elements has n-1 separator ends, all balance-0 + floored), so b is pinned by the consumer's explicit close hypothesis tokens[hi]!.val = .flowSequenceEnd — seqWindowRecSeqBody's fourth G-conjunct — not by the interior gate; re-probe a boundary-pinning attribution even when a settled pointer states it)
    • ProbeProofFactorsGeneralReduction — Reflection 468 (a native_decide/#guard satisfiability PROBE of P(concrete) whose proof factors as general_reduction (concrete_fact) is itself a TEMPLATE for the general producer — only the native_decide leaf is example-specific, so promote the probe by replacing that ONE leaf with a structural derivation of the single fact it localized, reusing the generic reduction VERBATIM. The toy of the seq-side residual-(2) discharge: seqFoldTotal_satisfiable_on_real_output (R441) native_decided the fold-totality universal ∀ m, ∃ s, btFold (some []) (take m) = some s on ONE stream — but its proof native_decided only the whole-object leaf WellTyped tokens.toList and then derived the ∀ m form GENERICALLY via WellTyped_prefix_some; so promoting it to the axiom-clean general producer (seqFoldTotal_of_context, retiring residual (2) of seqHRec_of_root_and_context) was just replacing that leaf with a structural derivation (seqWholeStreamWellTyped — fold the whole stream streamStart :: [ :: interior ++ ] :: streamEnd piecewise, the interior framing back via WellTyped_frame), reusing WellTyped_prefix_some verbatim. Toy: step/foldFrom a fold with an absorbing none (the btStep/btFold analog); Whole l := foldFrom (some 0) l = some 0 DECIDABLE (the PROBE's decide leaf); AllPrefixesFold l := ∀ m, ∃ s, foldFrom (some 0) (l.take m) = some s the deliverable; allPrefixesFold_of_whole THE producer-AGNOSTIC reduction (the WellTyped_prefix_some analog, consumes only Whole); probe_concrete := allPrefixesFold_of_whole _ (by decide) and general_producer l h_struct := allPrefixesFold_of_whole l h_struct share the reduction — probe_is_producer_at_decide_leaf : probe_concrete = general_producer _ (by decide) := rfl. NEGATIVE: an underflowing list [-1, …] is not Whole (decide) and its first prefix folds to none, so the reduction's whole-fact is load-bearing. Sharpens [[ref-probe-deferred-universal-before-producing]] (the probe's PROOF STRUCTURE, not just its yes/no, localizes the generalization residual) and is the promote-side dual of [[ref-metric-bridge-is-composition]] / [[ref-root-seed-recursive-producer-swap]])
    • UnclosableArmThreeDestinations — Reflection 612 (a case analysis over an implementation step hands you one goal per arm, and when an arm will not close the goal is in exactly one of THREE places — which the goal itself does not tell you, because all three read as "refute this arm". (1) REFUTABLE, fact in the CALLER: the step alone permits the arm, the caller's post-check rejects it, so hoist the fact into the lemma's statement as a hypothesis — the caller already has it, cost is one argument not a proof. (2) REFUTABLE, fact in a THREADED INVARIANT: an earlier step established a state relation that kills the arm; thread it, folding it into an existing guarded conjunct rather than adding a tuple slot ([[ref-inline-payload-to-index-it]]). (3) NOT REFUTABLE — the arm really fires, because the implementation accepts input the specification has no production for; that is a DEFECT, not proof work, and no vocabulary will close it. Destination 3 is the CHEAPEST to test and the most EXPENSIVE to discover late — one runtime probe on a concrete input separates it from 1 and 2 in seconds, versus however long you spend building frame vocabulary before failing to prove the arm. So probe before you thread. Toy scan (the step) / scanAll (the caller loop, whose [] arm IS the post-check) / prodB (the grammar), with bar accepted at every depth but producible only at depth 0. Destination 1: caller_supplies_depth (provable, by induction, its base case exactly the post-check) against step_alone_cannot_bound_depth (a single step happily leaves the depth nonzero, so no per-step lemma of that shape exists). Destination 2: the advance edge scan_clears_fresh + the refutation pct_dead_when_stale compose to no_pct_after_any_step — the arm is dead not because of anything in the lemma but because of a fact established one step earlier — with pct_fires_when_fresh witnessing that the invariant is LOAD-BEARING, not decoration.     • OrderVersusDistancePredicates — Reflection 616 (a scanner strictening written as a LOOKBACK over the token stream can express any ORDER rule — "token B may not follow token A" — and no DISTANCE rule — "there must be text BETWEEN them". The reason is structural, not a matter of reading further back: the emitted stream records what each token IS and where it STARTS, never its EXTENT, and the extent is exactly what separation is about. no_lookback_sees_abutment proves it (axiom-free up to propext): streamAbutting and streamSeparated project to the SAME emitted list — same classes, same recorded positions — yet abutment_differs, so no function of the stream can agree with abuts on both. The contrast is one line: twoPropsInARow (item 9e's rule) is twoPropsKinds ∘ map kind BY CONSTRUCTION, so twoPropsInARow_is_an_order_rule is a single rw and no analogous rewrite exists for abuts. The extent does live somewhere — inside the dispatch that produced the token, before emission — so a distance rule has exactly ONE home: FORWARD, at the producing dispatch, on the character its own walk stopped before; §2's forwardOk #guards pin it. That placement also explains why such a guard needs no context gate the way Reflection 615's adjacency guard does: nothing token-less can slip between a token and the character it stopped at. SECOND HALF (§3), the probe trap: two sibling productions differing only in their name's CHARACTER CLASS do not fail on the same inputs. [102] ns-anchor-char is ns-char minus the flow indicators, so &a"x" is ONE anchor named a"x"; [153] ns-tag-char also drops everything outside ns-uri-char, so !t"x" is a tag and then a SEPARATE double-quoted scalar abutting it. The reachable followers are the complements of the classes, so anchorGaps == ['[', '{'] while tagGaps == ['[', '{', '"', '\''], their difference is ['"', '\''], and the reverse difference is EMPTY — one sibling's gap strictly contains the other's, which is why probing the wrong one looks like a complete answer. Probe the CLASS DIFFERENCE, not the surface shape. §4 pins the blast radius as an iff: the order rule and order+distance rule differ on exactly the streams the order rule accepts whose tokens abut, a nonempty class.     Refutations & false targets (Reflections 218, 311, 345, 661, 668, 673, 674, 676, 682, 684) #

    • ProducerGuardedTrap — Reflection 218 (the producer-guarded-quantifier trap)

    • DownstreamDeriskRestoresUpstream — Reflection 311 (a downstream consumer's de-risk can FALSIFY a deliberately-narrowed UPSTREAM deliverable; when a minimal pair shows the dropped conjunct is the discriminator AND it is independent of the producer's other outputs, restore it in the PRODUCER — the toy of the floor restored to flowBracketBalance_backward_open_locate. R309 sized the locator's deliverable DOWN to its rebase consumer, dropping the Dyck FLOOR; R311 is the sequel — the opener-TYPE consumer re-opens it. Tokens are a delta stream d : Nat → Int with opener type ty : Nat → Bool; bal is cumulative-difference balance, so composition is free. Correction 1 (NEGATIVE, #guard-backed): the toy of [{}, ["9"]] (deltas [1, 1, -1, 0, 1, 0]) at window start a = 5 admits BOTH p = 4 (the true innermost [, ty = true) AND p = 1 (a spurious {, ty = false, body } , [ nets 0) under the THREE BARE facts (p < a, d p = 1, bal (p+1) a = 0) — and they DISAGREE on type (ty 1 ≠ ty 4), so no consumer of the bare existential can pin ty p; the FLOOR separates them (bal 5 5 = 0 ≥ 0 vs bal 2 3 = -1). This sharpens R309's [ ] [ pair (same TYPE there; DIFFERENT type here — which is exactly why the type brick forces the floor back). Correction 2 (POSITIVE floor_unique): adding the floor makes the located opener UNIQUE (any two floored witnesses coincide) by pure balance composition — if p < q then bal (p+1) (q+1) = 0 yet = bal (p+1) q + d q = (≥0) + 1, absurd; uniqueness ⇒ the gate's innermost type IS ty p. The floor is INDEPENDENT of the three facts (the minimal pair satisfies them yet only p = 4 is floored), so it cannot be re-derived at the consume site — the producer must deliver it. The DUAL of [[ref-backward-locator-mirrors-forward]] correction 2's size-DOWN: a deliverable is sized to the UNION of consumers, and a new consumer's de-risk can size it back UP. Sharpens [[ref-minimal-pair-extracts-the-gate]] and [[ref-probe-deferred-universal-before-producing]])

    • SeparatorBlindTypedInvariant — Reflection 345 (a bracket-typed invariant is SEPARATOR-BLIND, so a separator-placement fact is provably not a projection of it — the PROBE behind (i'-b-B2c-bodySucc-first-entry), R344's lone residual. The de-front works only if FlowBodyContent.bodySucc-at-first-entry-end is reconstructible CARRIER-FREE from the carried guards (FlowBodyWindow.wellTyped = the typed-bracket invariant WellTyped, and FlowBodyContentDeep). PROBE: REFUTED at the substrate level. The decisive mechanism is mechanical — WellTyped's per-token step btStep matches the separator .flowEntry under the _ => some s IDENTITY catch-all (WellBracketed.lean:906), the SAME branch a content scalar takes, so the fold is utterly insensitive to separator placement. A MINIMAL PAIR settles it: two adjacent content units WITHOUT vs WITH a separator have the SAME WellTyped value, yet differ on bodySucc-at-first-entry; FlowBodyContentDeep is VACUOUS on a separator-free window (no openers/separators), so the full carried-guard set holds on the unseparated member while bodySucc fails. Hence NO WellTyped l → bodySucc l can exist. Toy: Tok (content/sep/opn/cls); separator-blind step/fold/WT; firstSucc (toy bodySucc at k=0); minimal pair noSep/withSep. POSITIVE (wt_blind + firstSucc_sep): both lists are WT — the invariant cannot tell them apart — and the SEPARATED one has the successor fact. NEGATIVE (no_reconstruction + firstSucc_nosep + minimal_pair): the UNSEPARATED list is WT yet fails firstSucc, so ¬ ∀ l, WT l → firstSucc l = true; that non-existence is the substrate-level refutation — bodySucc is genuine EMISSION information, needing SafeBodyUnit. Sharpens R296 (no balance-free form → no typed-bracket-free form, via the explicit separator-blindness) + [[ref-type-blind-invariant-transports-via-converse-frame]] (separator-placement is the specific fact a type-blind invariant cannot CREATE); resolves the "if none" branch of [[ref-context-free-adapter-knot]] / [[ref-field-audit-localizes-knot]])

    • ResidueWasAStateNeverEntered — Reflection 661 (a deferred arm has THREE ways out, and the third gets missed: derive the shape (a missing production), refute it from what the machine did (a fact about the scan), or find that the arm describes a state the machine never enters. The third needs nothing about the input — but it is unavailable while the case carrying the arm NAMES no state, because a constructor with no fields asserts an absence and an absence supports neither a derivation nor a refutation (the_evidenceless_case_says_nothing — both answers constructible; named_state_empties_the_arm once the fact rides). Second lesson, and the one that costs: a vacuous slot filled with a DUMMY is exactly where the strengthening lands. While the case is free, any inhabitant satisfies it, so producers reaching the slot under an impossible hypothesis fill it with whatever is to hand and the impossibility goes unrecorded; give the case a fact and every dummy owes it (the_dummy_cannot_pay) while the ones that were vacuous all along pay by saying so (producer_by_vacuity, the_field_collected_a_debt — 14 dummies = 12 vacuities + 2 real arguments). Prefer nofun to a dummy at an unreachable slot: same length, states the fact. Third: the base case is a SPECIFICATION OF THE ENTRY POINT — a field the seed cannot discharge is a claim about the initial state and the initial state is code, so the failure reads two ways (the field is wrong, or the entry point is) and you choose by asking what the field MEANS. Here the entry point was wrong: the scan spent a column on the byte order mark, which §5.2 says spends none, so the first line sat one deeper than every line after it — ﻿a: 1⏎b: 2﻿a: 1⏎b: 2 dedented below its own [187] l+block-mapping and was REFUSED, and ﻿---﻿--- was off column 0 and so not [203] c-directives-end at all but a plain scalar (a_column_of_drift_dedents, the_marker_is_lost_by_the_same_one — two unrelated-looking failures, one arithmetic; unmarked_inputs_cannot_see_it is why a suite can miss it for a whole campaign). §4: the field's disjunction is not a weakening but the two producer FAMILIES named, each total on its own side, with the consumer's own inFlow = false selecting one ([[OptionalFieldBuysDomain]] is the case where producers genuinely differ). The companion of [[RefutationOwesTheBranch]] — 660 owns the BRANCH a check takes, this owns the STATE the machine is in — and of [[UnusedAlternativeIsUnchecked]], where an alternative nothing produces is an alternative nothing has checked.     • StopSetMeetsAcceptClass — Reflection 668 (a deferring arm's survivors are an INTERSECTION of two sets: the park's stop set — what the producer's validation left possible on the rest of the line, carried as the pending's line fact — and the consumer's accept class — what the dispatcher returns .ok on. Compute the intersection once instead of refuting the stop set's members one at a time. At an enumerable dispatch the two methods coincide (tailSuffix_cap_block_empty/nodeStop_cap_block_colon — item 36's three refutations WERE the intersection, walked member by member); at the CONTENT dispatch the class is unbounded — every ns-plain-first character — so no enumeration exists, but the class's DEFINING GUARDS (¬indicator, ¬white, ¬break, printable, ¬BOM, plus the seven construct heads as an exception list) are exactly what an intersection proof consumes: each stop-set family contradicts one guard and the exceptions are checked individually, so the proof's size is guards + exceptions, independent of the class's cardinality (tailSuffix_cap_content_empty, nodeStop_cap_content_colon). The asymmetry worth noticing: the two sets are produced at DIFFERENT times by DIFFERENT code — the stop set at the previous step, the accept class at the current one — neither written to refute the other, and no new runtime check is needed; the refutation is the observation that two independently-shipped sets barely overlap. And the survivors must be shown INHABITED (§4), else the "narrowing" might be an emptiness in disguise and the arm it leaves a phantom ([[PhantomBranchNotADeferral]]'s question asked of a refutation).     • UnpayableArmIsABugReport — Reflection 682 (a deferred arm has a FOURTH way out past Reflection 661's three, and it is the one nobody looks for: the arm is INHABITED, the grammar has no derivation for it, and therefore the MACHINE is wrong. The other three exits all leave the runtime alone — derive the shape, refute it from what the scan did, find the arm names a state never entered — and each is unavailable once the arm's inputs exist, reach it, and are ACCEPTED (cannot_derive/cannot_refute/not_vacuous, then the_machine_is_wrong). What makes the exit visible is LOCATION: the unlocated residue mentions no machine at all (unlocated_mentions_no_machine) so it is a statement about the grammar, true of a corrected scanner and a broken one alike and testable in neither; the located one IS the check's firing condition read as a predicate on the state (located_is_the_check), so it names the missing derivation, the input that exhibits it, and the conjunction to test, all at once — and it discriminates (located_discriminates). Because check and arm are the same predicate, the narrowing does not make the arm unreachable but CONTRADICTORY, so the residue deletes rather than shrinks (arm_empties, narrowing_keeps_the_readable: the delta is exactly the arm). The sequel to [[ResidueWasAStateNeverEntered]] and the converse of [[LeniencyLivedInAGate]] — there the refusing check existed and a gate exempted the family, here no check existed and the PROOF is what reported it. A census of construction sites cannot settle it, because a transport site and a birth site are the same text.     Invariants, floors & asymmetries #

    Balance, floors & Dyck (Reflections 288, 296, 298–300, 304, 306, 312, 314, 368) #

    • GuardEdgeFloorAsymmetry — Reflection 288 (the DESCEND edge completes R287's guard-threading skeleton, and its lesson is that the SAME guard field needs DIFFERENT invariant strengths on different recursion edges — an instance of [[ref-converse-forward-invariant-asymmetry]] within one recursion. bal f a b = f b − f a toys flowBracketBalance; advance_dyck (toy of flowBodyWindow_advance's dyck) derives the tail dyck ≥ 0 from JUST the OUTER dyck ≥ 0 because the tail re-bases onto a balanced prefix (bal lo (m+1) = 0); descend_dyck (toy of flowBodyWindow_descend's dyck) needs the strictly-stronger FLOOR bal lo i ≥ 1 because the interior sits a level DOWN (the opener pushes the balance to 1, bal (k+1) i = bal lo i − 1) — the floor being what flowBracketBalance_matching_close threads through its forward scan, NOT recoverable from the guard's outer dyck. POSITIVE on a real matched bracket fNest = "[ [ ] ]": nest_offset/nest_floor/nest_interior_dyck (floor holds ⇒ interior dyck follows). NEGATIVE on fFlat = "[ ] [ ]": flat_outer_dyck (outer dyck ≥ 0 holds everywhere) yet flat_interior_dips (bal fFlat 1 2 = −1 dips below 0) and not_flat_interior_dyck (the descend conclusion is outright FALSE), because flat_floor_fails (bal fFlat 0 2 = 0, not ≥ 1) — so the advance edge's weaker hypothesis cannot be reused on the descend edge; #guards confirm both edges strictly narrow the width metric)
    • NonRestrictionResidualNeedsRootSeed — Reflection 296 (a non-restriction residual needs a ROOT SEED, not a stronger local invariant — the toy of flowBodyContent_bodySucc_of_part6, the certain root-seed half of (i'-b). The recursion's guards thread cleanly only because their fields are all-depth and balance-FREE, making each edge a pure SUBSET restriction; a depth-0-balance-keyed field (bodySucc) has no such form, so no LOCAL carrier descends. POSITIVE afterOpener_restrict: an all-depth AfterOpener (toy of FlowBodyContentDeep's opener/separator fields) restricts to any sub-window by omega — the three-line descend/advance edges. NEGATIVE parent_holds ∧ child_fails: the depth-0 Depth0Sep (toy of bodySucc) holds on the parent window [0,4) of wt = opn scal scal cls ([ a b ], its only balanced-prefix end the close at k=3) yet FAILS on the descended interior [1,3) (there bal 1 2 = 0 fires on the scalar at k=1 — the child's depth-0 = the parent's depth-1, which the parent never speaks of — not followed by a separator); same tok, parent holds, child fails, so it is NOT a subset restriction. POSITIVE bridge_checked_to_bang: the root-seed move — infra delivers the fact with bounds-checked indexing T[k]'h, the consumer wants panic T[k]!, the bridge is a pure getElem!_pos conversion. The lesson: when a needed fact is provably NOT a subset-restriction, do not chase a stronger local invariant — seed its ROOT instance from existing infra now, and recognize the descended instances as a bracket-TYPE-context problem)
    • WindowAbsoluteGateSubsetRestriction — Reflection 298 (a WINDOW-ABSOLUTE gate makes the carrier a SUBSET restriction — the toy of SeqInteriorSeparators and its three-line _narrow/_descend/_advance edges. When a guard conjunct quantifies over sub-windows [a,b) ⊆ [lo,hi) under a gate, push the outer origin OUT of the quantifier body into the domain bounds: a body keyed only on a,b (never lo/hi) restricts to any sub-interval verbatim, so ONE monotonicity lemma is the whole descend/advance edge; a body that re-bases a fact on the origin BREAKS under narrowing (the ref-non-restriction-residual-root-seed failure). Gated positions gate a := a % 2 = 0. POSITIVE carrierAbs_narrow: the abstract window-absolute carrier ∀ a b, lo ≤ a → a ≤ b → b ≤ hi → gate a → factAbs a restricts to [lo',hi') by two Nat.le_trans — the entire edge. POSITIVE abs_survives_narrowing: carrierAbsB 0 4 = true ∧ carrierAbsB 1 4 = true (the window-absolute fact a % 2 = 0 survives moving the origin 0 → 1). NEGATIVE rel_breaks_under_narrowing: the origin-relative fact factRel lo a := (a - lo) % 2 = 0 gives carrierRelB 0 4 = true (offsets {0,2,4} of the gated even positions are even) but carrierRelB 1 4 = false (the SAME gated positions {2,4} now have odd offsets {1,3} — #guard (2-0)%2=0 vs (2-1)%2=1 is the re-basing flip). The lesson: choosing where the window origin lives is the whole game — origin in the bounds ⇒ subset restriction for free; origin in the body ⇒ a per-edge descent proof that may be impossible. The remaining cost is the ROOT seed, which no body-design makes free)
    • BoundaryResidualEndDual — Reflection 299 (the BOUNDARY residual is the INTERIOR fact's END-dual, discharged VACUOUSLY — the toy of SafeBodyUnit_last_not_sep / SafeBodyUnit_array_last_not_sep_window and the per-window discharge seqSeparatorFacts_of_windowed_safebodyunit. A producer-guard residual splits into an INTERIOR fact (every separator is followed by content) and a BOUNDARY fact (the body never ENDS in a separator). The boundary fact is the END-dual of the interior one over the SAME inductive substrate, and it discharges VACUOUSLY: the conclusion is unprovable directly — it asserts something about the token PAST the window, which the window's substrate cannot see — but it is reached only via a premise "the last in-window token is a separator", which the boundary lemma REFUTES, so the conclusion follows by absurd. Toy substrate UBody: positive "unit" tokens (content) separated by single 0s (the separator, like .flowEntry). POSITIVE UBody_ends_pos: the structural END-dual — every UBody is pre ++ [n] with 0 < n. POSITIVE UBody_last_not_sep (the faithful mirror of SafeBodyUnit_last_not_sep, same getElem?_append_right index bridge): the last token (k + 1 = length) is ≠ 0. POSITIVE UBody_noTrailing: assuming the last token IS the separator yields False — the vacuous discharge, the toy of noTrailingSepFact via absurd. Decidable model: ubodyB/endsInSepB; #guard ubodyB [3,0,5] ∧ !endsInSepB [3,0,5] (well-formed, no trailing separator) and NEGATIVE #guard !ubodyB [3,0,5,0] ∧ endsInSepB [3,0,5,0] plus !ubodyB [3,0] — a body ending in a separator is NOT well-formed, so the boundary the noTrailing premise speaks of is never reached (trailing_sep_rejected). The lesson: when a producer-guard residual splits into an interior and a boundary fact, suspect the boundary is the interior's END-dual over the same substrate, discharging vacuously by refuting that the boundary is a separator — prove the dual rather than survey derivability; the proof answers the question, lands the brick, and reveals both facts flow from one deliverable (seqSeparatorFacts_of_windowed_safebodyunit: one windowed SafeBodyUnit ⇒ both carrier facts))
    • PrefixGateReconstructedFromBoundary — Reflection 300 (a PREFIX-quantified gate is NEVER projectable from an interior-only window guard — the toy of enclosingMark_true_of_opener / seqTypedInterior_of_opener, the Q2 discharge for (i'-b-descend-root).     • RebaseFactFromEnclosingWindow — Reflection 304 (a window-relative carrier fact RE-BASES from its ENCLOSING window's same fact by balance-0 composition; the rebase precondition is load-bearing — the toy of bodySuccFact_rebase/noTrailingSepFact_rebase, the R303 redirect's first brick.     • CoerceToWeakerReuseWrapper — Reflection 306 (reuse the WEAKER structure's existing wrapper via a strong→weak COERCION, not a mirrored wrapper — the toy of SafeBodyUnit_safeBody + seqInteriorFeContentStart_of_windowed_safebodyunit, the (i'-b-encfacts) move.     • TypeBlindInvariantTransportsViaConverseFrame — Reflection 312 (a TYPE-BLIND numeric invariant (the floor) cannot CREATE a typed fact — fold-definedness — only LICENSE its transport across a frame boundary; moving definedness from a verified LARGER context to a SMALLER sub-context needs the CONVERSE of the forward framing lemma, not the forward one — the toy of seqOpenerType_of_located_and_gate / btFold_frame_inv. The 159th "Next step" predicted route (B): prove the singleton-frame interior fold returns to [b] from the floor, then extend by the FORWARD frame. Authoring corrected it. A 5-symbol typed-bracket alphabet (os/om push true/false, cs/cm pop a matching head or fail, nt neutral); step/fold the typed machine, pbal the numeric balance. Correction 1 (NEGATIVE, #guard-backed — the floor is type-blind): [os, cm] (the [ } analog) has every prefix balance ≥ 0 and total 0 (passes BOTH the floor and balance-0 that route (B) relied on) yet fold (some []) [os, cm] = none — the } cannot pop the [; so no floor/balance reasoning gives fold-definedness, a typed fact only the real stream supplies. Correction 2 (POSITIVE, the forward/converse asymmetry): fold_frame (forward) needs NO numeric hypothesis (carries a proven SMALL fold up); fold_frame_inv (the CONVERSE) DOES need the floor (transports DEFINEDNESS down — dropping frame elements would underflow unless the floor forbids it). step_frame_inv's 0 ≤ s.length + delta t is the no-pop-into-frame guard; the floor re-relativizes per step via step_length. The brick (opener_bit_via_converse_frame): the gate gives fold (some (b::s_p)) interior = some S (definedness in the LARGE context), fold_frame_inv (+ floor) pushes it down so the interior folds from [] to length-pbal-= 0 = [], hence S = b :: s_p — head = b, fixed by the gate. A property and its converse over one structure need DIFFERENT invariant strengths — forward free, converse floor-guarded ([[ref-converse-forward-invariant-asymmetry]]); the de-risk's route-(B) error was expecting the floor to do the FORWARD lemma's job. Sharpens [[ref-probe-deferred-universal-before-producing]])
    • TwoFloorRelayCloseBound — Reflection 314 (a located matching-close's containment bound is a TWO-FLOOR RELAY — a single underflow witness at close+1, refuted by whichever of two ADJACENT-domain floors tiles it — the toy of seqClose_of_located_and_enclosing. The descent locates the enclosing opener p and its matching close j (balance (p+1) j = 0, close delta -1); the consumer needs the close to CONTAIN the gated window [a,b) (a ≤ j ∧ b ≤ j), but the matching-close locator only returns j < hi. The bounds come from the consumer's OWN floors. The close underflows the next step for ANY base β ≤ j: balance β (j+1) = balance β j − 1. The span [p+1, b] is TILED by two separately-sourced floors — the locator floor over [p+1,a] and R313's gate floor over [a,b] — and you case-split on which domain close+1 lands in: a ≤ j (else j+1 ≤ a, locator floor gives balance (p+1)(j+1) = −1 < 0, contradiction), then b ≤ j (else j+1 ≤ b; having a ≤ j ⇒ a ≤ j+1, gate floor gives balance a (j+1) = −1 < 0 with balance a j = 0 by composition). No unified floor needed — adjacent floors that tile suffice; the first bound is the ordered GUARD for the second floor's lower edge. PROVEN (located_close_bounds, the abstract nugget over any bal with additivity + the close-underflow step): a ≤ j ∧ b ≤ j. POSITIVE (#guard) on W = op op ct cl op ct cl cl ([[1],[2]] skeleton, first inner seq p=1, close j=3): bal 2 3 = 0, d W[3] = −1, bal 2 4 = −1 (the close+1 underflow), genuine window [2,3) has a=2 ≤ 3 ∧ b=3 ≤ 3. NEGATIVE (#guard): the cross-sibling [2,6) is balanced but dips to bal 2 4 = −1 (gate floor over [2,6] fails) and 6 > j=3 — so b ≤ j would be FALSE without the gate floor. This is the general mechanism behind R313's "the floor discharges b ≤ hiS for free", generalized to two floors ([[ref-two-floor-relay-close-bound]]); the seq-specialized flowBracketBalance_matching_close_seq already bundled matching-close + typed-close (search the SPECIALIZED packaged lemma before composing the generic primitives, echoing [[ref-metric-bridge-is-composition]]))
    • StrictContainmentNeedsOpener — Reflection 368 (a two-floor relay closes only the NON-strict containment b ≤ c; the STRICT b < c — the inner close does not coincide with the enclosing interior's OWN end — needs a THIRD input the relay is HEAD-BLIND to: the inner window's START opener at a-1. The enclosing gate constrains the interior balance + Dyck floor + the mark at a, but says NOTHING about the token at a-1, so it cannot tell a genuine entry interior (opener [) from a MID-ENTRY SUFFIX (start one-past a separator ,): inside an interior x , [y] the window at a = one past the , passes the gate with b = the enclosing end c. So the relay alone yields b ≤ c; the opener bal (a-1) a = 1 upgrades it to b < c (it composes to bal (off+1)(a-1) = -1, contra the enclosing Dyck floor). The opener is INDEPENDENT of the gate's other fields (the mark gives the stack TOP at a, not the token at a-1), so it cannot be re-derived downstream — restore it as a window-absolute carrier field that descends free. POSITIVE = strict_containment, the abstract relay proved with the opener hypothesis; NEGATIVE = a concrete [ x , [y] ] balance where the mid-suffix window a=3 passes the gate (bal 3 6 = 0, floor ≥ 0) with b = c = 6 yet bal 2 3 = 0 ≠ 1 (opener absent), while the genuine window a=4 has bal 3 4 = 1 and b = 5 < 6. Real code: seqTarget_close_lt_interiorEnd (SeqInteriorSeparators.lean, R368, the descend re-bundle's win_hi).     • ProducerGuardedOracleReconstruct — Reflection 292 (discharge a producer-guarded oracle by RECONSTRUCTING the descended guard in place from the locator's own facts — never by re-running the locating descend-lemma, which finds its own witness j' and saddles you with a j = j' matching-close uniqueness proof; the toy of recseqentry_seqbracket_oracle. The oracle hands in the close j WITH the facts that pin it (lo+1 < j, bal l (lo+1) j = 0), so win_reconstruct builds the descended guard Win l (lo+1) j at THAT j directly — parametric in j, no re-location — and oracle_discharge feeds it to the IH (reconstruct_fires : P nested 1 3 on the nested bracket [opn,opn,cls,cls], the outer close j = 3). NEGATIVE: the naive re-locator firstClose nested = 2 finds the INNER close, not the outer matching close 3 the oracle was handed (#guard firstClose nested != 3), and bal nested 1 2 = 1 ≠ 0 shows the guard at that re-located witness targets the WRONG, unbalanced window — substituting the re-located witness is unsound without uniqueness, which reconstruct-in-place never incurs. SECOND FACET — the field the strengthened guard DROPPED resurfaces as a hypothesis: Deep (toy of FlowBodyContentDeep, balance only, the succ/bodySucc field dropped because its descend edge was unprovable) deep_holds on noSep = [opn,cls,val] yet deep_lacks_succ (the successor at 2 is val, not sep) — so the dispatch's trailing-separator successor is unrecoverable from Deep and must come from Full (toy of FlowBodyContent, carrying succ), full_supplies_succ. The lesson: locate ONCE in the consumer, RECONSTRUCT (don't re-locate) in the producer; and a guard's deliberately-shed field returns as the consumer's own owed hypothesis)
    • WidenedSlotPinnedBySibling — Reflection 619 (widening ONE conjunct of a conjunctive invariant buys nothing while a SIBLING conjunct still constrains the same variables — and the sibling that pins it is usually the one no consumer in that region reads. The accumulation invariant carried PendingNode false sp_start sp_flow sp_scan beside sc.flowLevel ≥ 1 → GStar SSWhite sp_flow sp_scan, the second added for ONE purpose: a flow-interior plain scalar ends its production before the whitespace collectPlainScalarLoop then consumes, so inside a flow collection the accumulator legitimately sits a run behind the cursor. That room was never occupied. Inside an open flow collection the only DERIVABLE PendingNode constructor is noPending : PendingNode false sp_start sp sp — every other one demands a stream- or document-level witness AT sp_flow, and an unclosed flow collection has none — so the widened slot was provably always GStar.nil, every producer discharged it that way, and the arm that needed it (accum_step_content's plain-scalar case at depth ≥ 1) could not be written at all. §1 pend_pins_the_endpoints is cases on a two-constructor inductive and is axiom-FREE, which is the point: the pin was never hard to see, it was never LOOKED at. §2 widened_slot_is_dead derives the equality from the invariant itself and no_room_for_the_step shows the failure's actual shape — not a hard goal but an EMPTY hypothesis. §3–§4 the repair: the pinning conjunct was not wrong, it was OVER-SCOPED. same_at_depth_zero is the whole soundness argument for re-scoping it to flowLevel = 0 (one line, an iff at the only depth anything reads it) and strictly_weaker_at_depth confirms the change is real, not a rename — the mirror of Reflection 617's inv'_is_strictly_stronger, where an invariant had to GROW to imply a new guard; here it SHRINKS to stop implying an old equation. §5–§6 the cheap diagnostic that would have found it: rescoping_is_sound over a three-site consumer table, computed from one grep for h_pending per branch — there_is_a_non_reading_site is accum_step_flow's flow-interior branch, fully proven and never mentioning the hypothesis.     • HeldTokenRepointsIndex — Reflection 620 (before widening a slot to hold something scanned-but-unattached, ask what ELSE in the invariant is COMPUTED from the same history — a held token re-points every index that is a function of it, and that breaks first. The plan was to widen the flow-interior gap so it could hold a scanned [96] c-ns-properties; the first thing that failed was not the gap but tailOf sc.tokens, the index NAMING the open frame. tailOf read the last real token, so the instant &a is scanned after [ the index moves to .colon while the frame is untouched — still betweenEmpty, indexed .sep — and there is no .colon-indexed empty frame, so the invariant is unsatisfiable BEFORE any vocabulary for the slot exists (§2 raw_index_unsatisfiable, axiom-FREE; frame_index_satisfiable is the same history under an index that reads past the run). The repair has a dual worth the reflection: the index was not decoration, it was TRANSPORTING two scanner guards — checkFlowAdjacency and scanFlowEntry both read the LAST real token, and the accumulation turned their success into tailOf … ≠ .value / ≠ .sep. An index that reads past a held run no longer reads what the guards read, so both transports go false, and §3 transport_fails_without_sync is the history that kills them ([a &b: the guard holds on the harmless &b while the frame's own token is the completed a; the scanner rejects that input, but the LEMMA cannot know it). §4 the fix: carry the COINCIDENCE of the two readings — Sync ts : frameIdx ts = rawIdx ts — as a conjunct, state both transports under it (transport_under_sync), and discharge it at each producer from the token it just pushed (sync_of_push). §6 shows the coincidence is strictly WEAKER than "no run is held" ([prop] alone holds a property and the readings still agree, both falling back to .colon) — which is why the invariant carries the coincidence and not the emptiness: the exactly-right hypothesis is the one the transports need. §5 is why this ordering pays: inv_embeds + props_case_is_new — with Sync already threaded, the slot's second constructor is purely ADDITIVE, since it simply omits the conjunct.     • AutoDetectedIsExistential — Reflection 647 (a production's "auto-detected" parameter is an EXISTENTIAL, and the scope it is bound at is half its content. Inline it as a constant and the formalized language silently SHRINKS — sound, complete-looking, and strictly smaller than the spec's (fixed_sound + fixed_incomplete); bind it inside the repetition instead of outside it and the language silently GROWS, admitting the ragged shapes the spec's "fixed m" forbids (ragged_complete + ragged_unsound). Same omission, opposite failures, so the parameter belongs on the constructor that OPENS the collection, threaded into the entries' shared index, not on the entries' own constructors. The tell is that nothing catches it: [183] l+block-sequence(n) and [187] l+block-mapping(n) are ( s-indent(n+m) … )+ for some fixed auto-detected m > 0, while SBlockSeqEntries n takes SIndent n per entry and SBlockNode.blockSeq passes seqSpaces n c exactly — so the - of a top-level sequence must sit at column 0, and - a / a:⏎ - x, most of the language, parse correctly with NO derivation at all. The constructor's own docstring says it, beside the production it cites: "Each entry = s-indent(n+1)" against a rule that says n+m. A differential sweep compares two ACCEPTORS and agrees with itself on exactly the inputs where the two disagree about derivability (gap_at_every_positive_indent); only reading the production against the inductive finds this. §3 measures the repair — 7 construction sites, ZERO elimination sites, so the widening itself is free ([[AwaitNotOpener]] §3) — and says why item 21 RECORDED the gap instead of making the edit: the consumer is blocked one level up, where two pendings pin the awaited node's indent to 0, and a constructor with no arm is the inhabitation debt this campaign refuses. Third time completeness debt has been in the surface GRAMMAR rather than the proof (items 9l, 20, 21).     • PinnedParameterFakesASideCondition — Reflection 648 (a pinned existential does not only shrink the language ([[AutoDetectedIsExistential]]); it propagates DOWNSTREAM as a side condition that reads like a fact about the domain. Every lemma about the wrapped construct has to be stated where the pinned value happens to be right, and the restriction is then recorded as a hypothesis with a plausible justification attached — one that survives review precisely because it is TRUE where it is stated (truth_does_not_separate_them). The tell is the SHAPE of the justification: arithmetic that happens to agree ("n-1 and n coincide at n = 0") is an artifact of a missing quantifier upstream, whereas a domain fact ("no producer reaches another value") is not, and only the first dissolves when the quantifier is restored (widening_does). SBlockNode_blockIn_to_blockOut was stated at n = 0 and its own comment gave the coincidence as the reason — seq-spaces(n,block-out) = n-1 against seq-spaces(n,block-in) = n, truncating to agreement at 0 — and with [183]'s m bound on the wrapper, m+1 absorbs the step and the lemma holds at EVERY index (relabel_pinned_fails_off_zero + relabel_widened), which is what let the mapping pending carry a nonzero one. Two further halves: the pinning also made four dispatch lemmas CASE on whether the whitespace run before the indicator was empty, deferring the other half at eight call sites — but the run is a measurement, its width IS the collection's indentation and nil is width 0, so reading it collapses the split at a cost of ZERO new arm bodies (§3); and a re-indexed carried closure needs a second measure beside [[AwaitNotOpener]]'s elimination count — of the consumers that must now produce at the new index, how many MENTION it, since the ones that do not ([72] e-node + [79] s-l-comments) transport verbatim and only the rest can block, which is what makes such a widening spendable in one item instead of waiting for a whole layer (§4).     • WideningIsAnOccurrenceQuestion — Reflection 649 (to widen a parameterized reading, ask WHERE the parameter occurs, not whether it can be weakened. Monotonicity is the wrong question twice over: the relation is often perfectly monotone in the OTHER direction, so the lattice reasoning is available and useless (antitone_holds beside the_needed_direction_fails), and a parameter is only as restrictive as the sub-productions that MENTION it. Locate the occurrences: if they all sit under one guard, the fragment on the other side re-reads at every value and the widening is a constructor rebuild rather than an induction over the family. A well-written grammar has already named that fragment — YAML's [111] nb-double-one-line, [122] nb-single-one-line, [133] ns-plain-one-line(c) are not an under-approximation of "reads at every index", they are EQUAL to it (index_free_iff), so look for the sibling production before writing a lemma. And the deciding measurement is usually already being taken, because guards are boundaries and boundaries are load-bearing more than once: §7.4 restricts an implicit KEY to one line and every occurrence of the indent in [161] ns-flow-node is on a continuation line, so item 15's s'.line = sc.line answers both — structurally, not by luck (one_measurement_two_consumers). Corollary for the escape count: when several by_cases in a row all fail into the same hatch with the same arguments they are ONE question with several negative answers, and splitting them inflates the call-site count while the domain stands still — [[CoverageNotCallSites]]'s inflation, authored rather than inherited (§4). Concretely [196] s-l+block-node(n,c)'s flow arm reads an entry's value at the ENTRY's index while every content reading was stated at 0, so - a had no derivation even after [[AutoDetectedIsExistential]] and [[PinnedParameterFakesASideCondition]] gave the collection its own. L4YAML DOCS item 23, 2026-08-11: the indented entry's one-line value composes; 3 siblings routed, 1 span induction, escape sites 13 → 13 with the family gone from the domain)
    • DifferentQuestionNotAHarderCase — Reflection 650 (a case that lands in your escape may be a DIFFERENT question, not a harder instance of yours — check that each negative case is even an INSTANCE of what you asked. A step of another shape has not answered "no"; it has failed to be asked, and filing it under the negative records the wrong obstruction, which is what schedules the next item. The bundling is invisible from the escape (same argument list either way) and from the suite (the input is accepted either way); it is visible only in the PROSE at the deferral site, where two families carry justifications of different KINDS — "this reading genuinely mentions the index" beside "this isn't a reading" (sound_on_the_domain beside the_misfiled_case: the two-answer form is CORRECT inside the domain, which is why it survives review). Separating it out is not a new branch for the caller but a third arm of the same disjunction, so each site keeps exactly one route to the escape while the escape's domain shrinks (refinement_shrinks_the_domain, genuine_negatives_unmoved). It pays because the unasked question can have a much better answer: [[WideningIsAnOccurrenceQuestion]] prices a widening by the parameter's occurrences, and the strongest answer is NO occurrence — what a freshly opened construct has, since it has not yet reached the sub-production the parameter lives in (fresh_run_reads_everywhere, with extension_across_a_break_pins showing the claim is not vacuous). And the honest measure moves sideways: the escape gained a site (13 → 14) and the distinct-cause count did not move, so what improved is what each cause is TRUE of — three shapes were filed under the route and only one was the route's, leaving a residue of three where two now share a cause (causes_converge, block_scalar_reclassified), which is the fact that says the next item is one item ([[CoverageNotCallSites]]). Concretely: item 23 recorded a property run among the four ways an indented value fails to read at every index, but & completes no value — it opens [96] c-ns-properties, which the accumulator PARKS, and the pinned 0 was in the pending's ROUTE, one step upstream of the reading.     • ConstantPreconditionIsUnmeasuredQuantity — Reflection 651 (a constant in a precondition is often a quantity you DECLINED TO MEASURE. When a hypothesis pins a coordinate to a literal — col = 0, n = 0, depth = 1 — ask where the literal came from: if its producer CASES on evidence it already holds and answers for only one shape of it (cases whites with | nil => build | cons => punt), the literal is not a requirement but the value you get by refusing to read what you have, and the fix is a conversion rather than a proof (refused_but_convertible). Distinguish it from a REAL side condition by whether the discarded arm has an answer at all: a tab where [63] s-indent wants spaces genuinely has none, and no reading makes one (genuine_punt, the_discriminator). Both punts are sound, both look identical at the call site (∨ True), and both leave the escape's site count untouched — only the domain differs, and the domain is the claim ([[CoverageNotCallSites]]). It hides because the constant attaches to the WRONG production: sp_key.col = 0 read as a fact about keys, and keys are exactly where it is not needed — [193] ns-s-block-map-implicit-key and [194] c-s-implicit-json-key take no indent at all, the spec writes n/a (head_is_index_free), so it was the ENTRY one production up that mentions the index and an entry takes any width (entry_at_any_width, with the old restriction exhibited as one instance of that family). The giveaway is that removing it needs no lift of the thing it named — [[WideningIsAnOccurrenceQuestion]]'s best case, no occurrence at all. Measuring is safe for the same reason [[AutoDetectedIsExistential]] said inlining was not: the width is bound ONCE where the collection opens and threaded to the entries (width_is_shared), so a sibling at another width is simply not this mapping. Concretely: items 15–17 built the whole vocabulary of [188]'s implicit key and admitted only the column-0 one, because keyctx_of_preprocess punted on residual whites that are [63] s-indent(k) — the entry's own indentation, read with the splitter item 22 already had.     • SideConditionNeedsItsQuantity — Reflection 652 (removing a pin returns a SIDE CONDITION, and a side condition is only as available as the QUANTITY it names. Un-pinning a parameter does not hand you the general statement; it hands you the general statement plus the arithmetic the constant used to satisfy for free (kept_gives_pinned beside consumer_needs_the_antecedent). So price the item by that condition's VOCABULARY rather than its difficulty: write it down and grep the invariant for each name in it, because a name with zero occurrences in CODE — prose mentions do not count and are the warning sign, since a quantity gets described in comments exactly when it is doing work nothing states — is the whole remaining item (cost_is_the_missing_quantity, with invariant_admits_a_counterexample showing the widening complete and the case still deferring).     • OptionalFieldBuysDomain — Reflection 653 (once [[SideConditionNeedsItsQuantity]] has named the quantity an invariant must start carrying, the remaining decision is what SHAPE to carry it in — and the default should be OPTIONAL. A required field obliges every producer of the constructor on the spot, so the ones that cannot measure must route their case to the escape, a route that did not exist before and that YOUR edit authored; carried as P ∨ True the same producer hands True back, the consumer keeps the single route it already had, and what moves is the domain (optional_costs_no_site, same_domain_fewer_sites). The coverage is identical either way — a producer that cannot measure could not have composed its case under either design — so the required form buys nothing and costs a site, which is [[WideningIsAnOccurrenceQuestion]] §4's inflation one layer up: there several by_cases failed into one hatch, here several PRODUCERS do. The optional field is not a proof and cannot be mistaken for one (punt_is_free beside punt_does_not_decide: Carried is inhabited at every index), and it transports without a special case at each re-park, absence included (carried_transport). The tell that you will need it: a family symmetric in the GRAMMAR need not be symmetric in the runtime — check, per producer, which coordinate the runtime actually pushes at, because a member that pushes at someone ELSE's coordinate cannot discharge the field locally (dash_and_key_push_at_their_own, value_pushes_at_its_own_iff_fresh, inherited_save_breaks_the_symmetry), and no reading of the productions would have flagged it (grammar_is_blind). Concretely: - pushes [183]'s indent at its own column and ? pushes [187]'s at its own, so both discharge IndentFloor; the : pushes at the RESOLVED KEY's column, its own only on a fresh save, so : | discharges and a: | hands True — a different coordinate, not a harder case ([[DifferentQuestionNotAHarderCase]]).     • PuntNamesACoordinate — Reflection 654 (an optional field's punt names a COORDINATE, not a difficulty. [[OptionalFieldBuysDomain]] leaves a record — every True is a producer you did not reach — and when you come back for one, read WHY it punted before trying to make its case harder-but-provable. If the runtime measures at a different coordinate than your lemma names, restate the PRODUCER's lemma at the coordinate the runtime uses: both lemmas describe the same push (pushedAt is one function, untouched), and a hypothesis about the used coordinate is dischargeable while one about an unused coordinate is not, at any strength (inherited_is_reachable beside inherited_unreachable_from_the_indicator). That makes the punt evidence of wrong UNITS, not of difficulty — [[DifferentQuestionNotAHarderCase]] one layer down, where a case was not an instance of the question and here it was not an instance of the measurement. Check both sides before building machinery: in a correspondence proof the same quantity is usually already carried on each side under different names (both_sides_already_had_it), so what is missing is the EQUATION, and the item is one conjunct that closes the consumer's bound by rewriting (equation_closes_the_bound) — itself optional, so the producers that cannot supply it keep their coverage (coupling_punt_is_free, coupling_punt_does_not_decide). And confirm the re-measurement does not RETIRE the old lemma: it trades domain, reaching producers that push at the new coordinate and losing those with no such coordinate to offer, so keep both as siblings when the domains are incomparable (neither_subsumes, together_they_are_total, indicator_lemma_still_needed).     • PuntMayBeALostProjection — Reflection 655 (a punt may be a LOST PROJECTION: read what the CARRIER kept, not only what the producer proved. [[PuntNamesACoordinate]] covers the punt whose producer measured the wrong coordinate; this one covers the punt whose producer measured the right thing and whose pack stored a SIBLING FIELD of it — a carrier holding pos.line when its producer proved pos = currentPos has thrown the column away, and the consumer's missing hypothesis is one field further back, not a proof gap (carrier_lost_it — two states the pack cannot tell apart disagree on what the consumer asks — beside producer_had_it, which is rfl). The two losses are INDISTINGUISHABLE at the consumer (the_consumer_cannot_tell_them_apart) and cost different things: a wrong coordinate needs the producer's lemma re-stated, a lost projection needs a wider carrier whose new field is discharged by the fact already proved (widened_field_is_the_old_fact and widening_is_additive, both rfl) — so classify on the PRODUCER's side (classification_is_a_producer_side_reading). An optional field's Trues are then a TAXONOMY, not a list of difficulties: LOST PROJECTION (widen the carrier), UNSTATED LEMMA (true and provable but stated nowhere the consumer can reach — typically the general version sits below its consumer in the file, so write a small dedicated sibling beside its peers rather than hoisting it) and DIFFERENT PRODUCTION, the only kind that is real work ([[DifferentQuestionNotAHarderCase]]) — and the taxonomy PREDICTS the item: the punts that closed are exactly the ones whose cause is bookkeeping (taxonomy_predicts_the_item). Finally, when the coupling becomes total, RECORD it rather than re-shaping it: making the now-total optional field required covers the same producers today (same_coverage_today) and refuses the next one that cannot measure (optional_admits_the_next_producer beside required_refuses_it), which is the inflation [[OptionalFieldBuysDomain]] declined.     • UnusedAlternativeIsUnchecked — Reflection 659 (an alternative nothing PRODUCES is an alternative nothing has CHECKED. A constructor that compiles has had its TYPES checked; its numeric arguments — an index, a width, an offset — are constrained by nothing until something instantiates them, so a production alternative with zero producers and zero consumers carries arithmetic with zero evidence behind it however long it has sat in the tree, however carefully it is annotated, however often the comments cite it (nothing_had_checked_it; the_two_never_agree — the error is a constant, not an edge case). And the first consumer is not the test: an off-by-one in a COLLECTION's index is invisible on a collection of one, because the opener's position is supplied by the opener, and becomes visible on the SECOND element, whose indentation the index is what decides — so the pin to write first when a dormant alternative wakes up is the two-element case (agrees_without_a_sibling beside one_sibling_tells, one_element_is_not_a_check). Why it was dormant is the second lesson: the escape it closes had been described for a dozen items as an EVIDENCE gap — the step crosses no break, so [79] s-l-comments cannot exist, so nothing can close, irreducible — and that description is true of the alternatives already CONSUMED. Read the production's alternative list instead and the partition is stark: the consumed set coincided EXACTLY with the set that demands the missing evidence (consumed_is_exactly_the_evidence_demanding_set, one cases), so on the residue every consumed alternative was unavailable and every available one unconsumed (residue_has_no_consumed_alternative beside residue_is_not_empty). A gap that looks irreducible from inside the consumed set is worth re-reading as "which alternatives do NOT ask for the thing I cannot get". What kept it unexpressible was an INTERFACE, not a proof: the pending's entry-level closure was an ACCUMULATOR — here is what has been accumulated, here is how to spend any extension — and that shape obliges the producer to name a BEGINNING, which a compact collection has not got, its first entry not being preceded by its own indentation and so not an element of the collection type at all (compact_opener_is_not_an_entry, accumulator_needs_a_beginning). The CONTINUATION shape — give me the rest and I close — names no beginning, admits the empty rest (continuation_admits_the_empty_rest) and agrees with the accumulator wherever both exist (indented_opener_spends_the_tail), so prefer it: strictly more producers at strictly no call sites (continuation_is_strictly_wider). Concretely: [185] s-l+block-indented(n,c) has four alternatives and the accumulation consumed the two that need comments, never touching s-indent(m) ns-l-compact-sequence(n+1+m) or its mapping twin — which are precisely the two that need none, a compact collection sharing the entry indicator's line. Both had been written n+m, one column left of the truth.     • PuntMayBeTheBoundary — Reflection 665 (a punt may be the BOUNDARY of the family, not debt. [[PuntMayBeALostProjection]] sorts an optional field's Trues into lost projection, unstated lemma and different production — three kinds, all of them work; there is a fourth that is not, a site where the datum is FALSE, so closing the punt would be unsound rather than expensive. Two questions separate the four, in order: does any input REACH the site (if none does it is a phantom and retires, [[PhantomBranchNotADeferral]]), and if some does, can you prove the datum's NEGATION at that input — [[VacuityIsAClaimAboutTheRuntime]]'s cheap experiment run on the other side, refuting "unreachable" there and confirming "unpayable" here (phantom_has_no_input, root_is_debt, indented_is_a_boundary, and closing_the_boundary_is_refutable — there is no such function at any price). The type cannot tell them apart and should not: all three punts are the same term (the_type_cannot_tell), so the classification is a reading of the SITE (classification_is_a_site_reading), and the boundary is not a hole because the deferral still serves the input (every_landing_is_served). Where the boundary is already written down: in the SIDE CONDITION of the route the site would have to build — a frame lemma taking n ≤ k has said which landings it serves before any producer exists, so the punt list is DERIVED, not discovered by trying arms (the_side_condition_is_the_family, the_root_half_is_free, punts_are_computed); [[EqualityGateHidesTwoOrders]] is the same arithmetic one step earlier, while the gate is still an equality. L4YAML item 39: nestedBlockMap's n ≤ k is vacuous at the root value, so k:⏎ a: 1 composes through valueMapRoute — the third producer of item 38's pack, costing one frame lemma and zero consumer edits — and false at an indented pendingMapValue, where a landing may be a DEDENT ( : v⏎a: 1 ends the enclosing entry rather than nesting inside its value), so the DEDENT keeps its True permanently — item 40 then found that the ARM does not, the two halves being decidable where the pack is built ([[PuntTheShapeNotTheSite]]). Continues [[CarryTheRouteNotTheCoordinates]]: that item made the second producer possible, this one measures what the third cost)
    • PuntTheShapeNotTheSite — Reflection 666 (punt the SHAPE, not the site. [[PuntMayBeTheBoundary]] classifies an optional field's Trues by asking two questions of the SITE — is it reached, and does the datum's negation go through at something that reaches it — and a site can answer YES to both (the_site_is_mixed). Its inputs then split, and calling it a boundary draws the boundary in the wrong place: an arm-level punt serves nothing that reaches the arm, so every landing on the TRUE side is lost with the dedent (the_arm_serves_none beside the_shape_serves_the_true_half, shape_dominates, shape_is_strictly_wider). The question that separates a mixed site from a real boundary is not "can I prove the datum here" but "is the datum a FUNCTION of what this arm already knows" — if it is, so is its negation, and the by_cases is the whole payment (the_side_condition_is_decidable, produce_is_real_where_it_nests); run isMixed before writing the punt, since a site all of whose landings refute is a genuine boundary and splitting it buys nothing (a_real_boundary_is_not_mixed). What makes a mixed site visible is MERGING: two producers that differ only in which branch of one disjunction they take are one producer with a case split in it, and while they are apart a consumer holding a single optional field must CHOOSE one, capping coverage at the better branch instead of the union (neither_alone_is_total, merged_is_total, the_reading_does_not_branch) — with the frame only one caller can offer entering as an optional argument, free to the other (optional_frame_costs_the_other_caller_nothing). L4YAML item 40, 2026-08-16: entryKeyPack_of_dispatch is items 38 and 39's producers merged at the disjunct the preprocessing already returns, so a --parked pending gains the break-crossed frame (-⏎ a: 1) and item 39's permanent punt at the indented mapping value becomes the dedent's alone (k:⏎ :⏎ a: 1 composes, k:⏎ :⏎b: 2 defers); pack producer lemmas 2 → 1 and 175 lines → 128, call sites 3 → 4, mixed-site arm punts 1 → 0, zero new route or frame lemmas, zero consumer edits, escape sites 6 → 6 and scannerDrop 4 → 4)
    • CoverageIsBranchTimesFrame — Reflection 667 (a call site's coverage is its BRANCH times its FRAME. Once a producer is merged over a case split ([[PuntTheShapeNotTheSite]]) and each branch needs a different optional argument, a site is served on a branch only if the caller's evidence lemma can REACH that branch and the caller's pending can OFFER the frame it needs — so covers = sees && offers ∘ needs, and the product is computable from types alone before any call is written, because which branches a caller sees is a property of the lemma it destructured (a break-free analysis returns one) and which frames it offers is a property of its slot (SBlockIndented has a compact alternative, SBlockNode does not). Reading either factor alone gets a cell wrong (neither_factor_alone_predicts), and an empty product is silent: the call typechecks and returns True forever. An empty cell is NOT a boundary — the two questions of [[PuntMayBeTheBoundary]] and the split of [[PuntTheShapeNotTheSite]] both need a datum that is FALSE somewhere, and nothing here is false: each factor of the empty product is shared with a site that IS served (the_branch_factor_is_not_fatal, the_frame_factor_is_not_fatal), so the emptiness belongs to the PAIR (emptiness_is_a_property_of_the_pair) and relaxing either factor closes it (either_relaxation_closes_it), which a boundary never permits. Corollary in the same table: the route's side condition — items 39's boundary and 40's decidable split — is VACUOUS here, because the only sites that reach the branch testing it carry index 0 (the_side_condition_is_vacuous_where_it_can_fire), so one inequality is a boundary, a shape or nothing depending only on which sites reach it.
